magandang hapon po sa ating lahat.

**[00:02:22.140] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000028)  
Allow me to begin by...

**[00:02:24.300] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000029)  
putting the CHR budget in perspective through one figure

**[00:02:27.860] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000030)  
that I hope will remain with this committee throughout this day,

**[00:02:31.800] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000031)  
deliberation of 0.02%.

**[00:02:36.300] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000032)  
Sa kabuang 7.2 trillion proposed national budget for 2027,

**[00:02:43.780] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000033)  
the Commission on Human Rights is provided 1.273 billion pesos

**[00:02:50.460] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000034)  
under National Expenditure Program,

**[00:02:53.200] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000035)  
or just 0.02% of the entire national budget.

**[00:02:58.960] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000036)  
The CHR, however, proposed a total budget of 2.749 billion pesos.

**[00:03:05.120] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000037)  
Even if Congress were to provide this entire amount,

**[00:03:08.860] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000038)  
it will still account for only around 0.04% of the national budget,

**[00:03:14.480] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000039)  
from 0.02% provided under the NEP,

**[00:03:17.840] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000040)  
to 0.04% if the entire CHR proposal were funded.

**[00:03:24.000] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000041)  
Ganoon po kaliit ang hinihing bahagi ng Commission on Human Rights

**[00:03:29.180] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000042)  
mula sa ating pambansang pundo.

**[00:03:31.120] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000043)  
Yet the responsibility entrusted to this constitutional institution

**[00:03:36.120] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000044)  
is anything but small.

**[00:03:39.380] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000045)  
The CHR remains among the lowest funded institution of the national government,

**[00:03:45.560] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000046)  
yet its mandate extends across the country

**[00:03:48.700] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000047)  
and touches the lives and rights of every Filipino.

**[00:03:53.200] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000048)  
We expect the Commission to investigate,

**[00:03:56.120] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000049)  
allege human rights violations and assist victims.

**[00:03:59.520] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000050)  
We expect it to monitor jails and other places of deprivation of liberty,

**[00:04:06.020] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000051)  
help prevent torture and abuse,

**[00:04:08.920] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000052)  
educate our people about their rights,

**[00:04:12.180] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000053)  
and advise Congress and government on human rights laws and policies.

**[00:04:18.060] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000054)  
Habang lumalaki po at nagiging komplikado ang haman sa karapatang pantao,

**[00:04:23.200] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000055)  
kailangan din po natin tiyakin na meron po tayong sapat na kakayanan

**[00:04:28.440] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000056)  
at resources ang institusyong inaatasan ng ating konstitusyon para tumugon sa mga ito.

**[00:04:35.420] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000057)  
Mga kasama, we cannot continue to demand more from our constitutional institution

**[00:04:41.040] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000058)  
without ensuring that it has the resources necessary

**[00:04:46.480] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000059)  
to effectively perform the responsibilities to entrust it.

**[00:04:50.760] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000060)  
And let us also be careful.

**[00:04:53.200] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000061)  
We need to be clear.

**[00:04:54.160] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000062)  
The CHR is not advisory of government.

**[00:04:58.200] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000063)  
Kakampi po natin ang CHR sa mabuting pamamahala.

**[00:05:02.680] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000064)  
Ang independent and adequately funded CHR helps government identify gaps,

**[00:05:09.660] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000065)  
prevent abuses, protect vulnerable sectors, and strengthen accountability.

**[00:05:15.280] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000066)  
When the CHR tells us where government can do better,

**[00:05:18.840] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000067)  
it helps us build stronger and more accountable,

**[00:05:23.200] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000068)  
more responsible institutions.

**[00:05:25.200] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000069)  
This is why the budget before us should not be viewed simply as funding for another government office.

**[00:05:32.700] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000070)  
When we invest in CHR, we invest in justice, accountability, good governance, and human dignity.

**[00:05:41.700] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000071)  
Kaya habang sinisimulan po natin ang debilirasyon ito,

**[00:05:45.700] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000072)  
I ask my distinguished colleagues, tatatlo po tayong nandito ngayon,

**[00:05:52.500] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000073)  
Consider not only how much? Apat na po ba?

**[00:05:57.039] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000073-split-2)  
Apat na.

**[00:05:59.800] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000074)  
consider not only how much we can afford to provide the CHR,

**[00:06:04.340] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000075)  
but also what we expect the Commission to accomplish with the resources we give.

**[00:06:10.120] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000076)  
After all, while CHR is provided only 0.02% of our national budget,

**[00:06:16.040] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000077)  
the right it is constitutionally mandated to protect belongs to

**[00:06:22.500] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000078)  
one hundred percent of the Filipino people.

**[00:06:24.860] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000079)  
With that, may I request the Chairperson of the Commission on Human Rights,

**[00:06:29.760] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000080)  
Chairperson Richard Palpal frase

**[00:06:34.300] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000081)  
to begin with their presentation.

**[00:06:36.300] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000082)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[00:06:38.300] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000083)  
Thank you Your Honour.

**[00:06:40.300] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000084)  
The Honorable Chairperson,

**[00:06:42.300] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000085)  
Distinguished Members of the Committee on Appropriations,

**[00:06:47.300] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000086)  
fellow public servants and everyone present today.

**[00:06:49.300] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000087)  
Magandang hapon po sa ating lahat.

**[00:06:51.300] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000088)  
I am pleased to present the Commission on Human Rights proposed budget for fiscal year 2027 along with our accomplishment for 2025 and the first semester of 2026 and targets for 2027.

**[00:07:04.980] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000089)  
For most, I would like to thank our budget sponsor, Representative Maria Cristina Talavera Lopez, for her opening statement and continued advocacy and support for the CHR.

**[00:07:16.720] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000090)  
The CHR's proposed budget aims to strengthen the Commission's delivery of services in line with its constitutional mandate for human rights protection and promotion,

**[00:07:28.400] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000091)  
prevention of human rights violations in places of deprivation of liberty, and policy advisory services to strengthen the Philippine government's compliance with its human rights obligations.

**[00:07:38.600] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000092)  
In terms of operations, the CHR has 726

**[00:07:46.720] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000093)  
out of 836 plantilla positions across the central office and 17 regional offices as of August 2026. With the permission of the Chair,

**[00:07:58.400] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000094)  
Let me begin by sharing the CHR's budget allocation and utilization rate.

**[00:08:03.900] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000095)  
Historically, the CHR's annual obligation rate has averaged 95% since 2019.

**[00:08:11.280] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000096)  
The CHR strives to maintain its disbursement rate, which has stood at 100%.

**[00:08:16.720] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000097)  
Since 2024, and by the end of fiscal year 2026, it is estimated that the CHR will be able to maintain similar budget, obligation, and disbursement rates.

**[00:08:28.600] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000098)  
Now, allow me to present a detailed breakdown of the CHR's proposed budget for the fiscal year of 2027 in comparison with the 2027 National Expenditure Program.

**[00:08:40.840] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000099)  
As shown in column 3, the CHR's original proposed budget for 2027 is 2.749 billion pesos.

**[00:08:46.720] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000100)  
A total of 1.173 billion was proposed under Tier 1, while 1.576 billion was proposed under Tier 2.

**[00:09:04.240] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000101)  
The CHR's total allocation under the 2027 NEP has been reduced by almost 53%,

**[00:09:11.560] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000102)  
which is equivalent to 1,272,592,000 pesos.

**[00:09:18.100] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000103)  
As shown in column No. 5, there is a deficit of 1.477 billion in the 2027 NEP proposal compared to the CHR's proposal under Tiers 1 and 2.

**[00:09:37.780] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000104)  
And this deficit mostly accounts for the Commission's capital outlay for 2027.

**[00:09:43.940] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000105)  
As such, the breakdown of the CHR's 2027 NEP

**[00:09:48.100] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000106)  
is as follows.

**[00:09:49.940] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000107)  
718,805,000 for personal services plus an additional 61.33 million for RLIP,

**[00:10:03.780] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000108)  
or a total of 780,138,000.

**[00:10:09.280] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000109)  
And for the maintenance and other operating expenses, 492,454,000.

**[00:10:18.100] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000110)  
Of course, the CHR is grateful to the Department of Budget and Management

**[00:10:22.160] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000111)  
for recognizing two of the Commission's important Tier 2 requirements.

**[00:10:28.120] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000112)  
As seen in the lower right-hand corner of the presentation,

**[00:10:32.900] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000113)  
Andiyan po, binigyan po tayo ng DBM ng 16,019,000

**[00:10:40.120] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000114)  
for the visitorial mandate of the CHR through the expanded Places of Deprivation of Liberty,

**[00:10:48.100] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000115)  
visitation program,

**[00:10:50.260] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000116)  
at 19,300,000 out of the 59 million for the augmentation of mandatory expenses

**[00:10:58.820] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000117)  
for central and regional offices.

**[00:11:02.440] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000118)  
To provide further context, the next slide shows,

**[00:11:08.840] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000119)  
compared with the 2026 GAA budget of 1.347 billion,

**[00:11:15.840] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000120)  
the 2027 NEP,

**[00:11:18.100] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000121)  
that only amounts to 1,272,592,000.

**[00:11:26.100] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000122)  
May I invite you to please look at the figures listed in the last column.

**[00:11:31.660] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000123)  
As seen in the row marked as grand total,

**[00:11:36.540] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000124)  
there's an overall decrease of 74,882,000 out of the budget of the CHR.

**[00:11:43.800] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000125)  
The decrease is primarily due to the lack of capital outlay allocation

**[00:11:47.800] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000126)  
in the 2027 NEP.

**[00:11:49.800] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000127)  
For MOOE, there is a decrease amounting to a negative variance of 34,888,000.

**[00:11:56.800] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000128)  
And for personal services, there's an increase amounting to 103,549,000

**[00:12:05.360] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000129)  
due to the increase in the filled-up positions of the CHR

**[00:12:10.800] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000130)  
and the implementation of the fourth tranche of the updated salary standardization law,

**[00:12:16.960] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000131)  
and

**[00:12:17.800] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000132)  
automatic appropriations for retirement or RLIP.

**[00:12:19.800] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000133)  
Now allow me now to illustrate how resources translate into actual services.

**[00:12:29.840] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000134)  
The CHR's constitutional mandate is discharged through the delivery of its programs in human rights protection,

**[00:12:36.080] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000135)  
prevention of human rights violation, human rights promotion and policy advisory services.

**[00:12:41.840] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000136)  
So, for protection services, the CHR focuses on the documentation and management of complaints,

**[00:12:48.840] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000137)  
human rights violations, and general policies.

**[00:12:49.840] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000138)  
So, for protection services, the CHR focuses on the documentation and management of complaints, human rights violations, and general policies.

**[00:12:50.840] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000139)  
independent forensic services, legal assistance and counseling, financial assistance and witness protection, or security.

**[00:13:00.460] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000140)  
In the first six months of 2026, the Commission received 2,942 cases of which 2,623 were already disposed or acted upon as of June 2026.

**[00:13:15.320] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000141)  
Ang breakdown po nito ay complaints received, meaning request for assistance, case investigation, and other protective services.

**[00:13:33.140] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000142)  
So through the Task Force Zero Backlog, the CHR has disposed of or acted upon 27,307 out of the 28,519

**[00:13:45.320] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000143)  
Backlog cases, leaving a total of 1,212 pending cases from 1987 to 2025.

**[00:13:54.820] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000144)  
This represents substantial progress in addressing long-standing cases of human rights violations.

**[00:14:00.860] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000145)  
Now for the prevention services of the Commission.

**[00:14:10.240] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000146)  
So the CHR is mandated to conduct regular visitation and conduct preventive monitoring of human rights violations.

**[00:14:17.340] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000147)  
conditions

**[00:14:18.800] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000148)  
In places of deprivation of liberty such as jails, military and police custodial facilities, prison facilities, child-carest facilities and mental health facilities, among others.

**[00:14:29.780] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000149)  
For visits in places of detention, the Commission conducted 960 POD visits in 2024 and in 2025, 985.

**[00:14:44.480] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000150)  
and the

**[00:14:46.260] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000158)  
for the first semester.

**[00:14:46.820] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000159)  
2026,

**[00:14:48.900] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000160)  
the CHR has visited

**[00:14:50.220] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000161)  
617 facilities.

**[00:14:54.420] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000162)  
Under the Commission's

**[00:14:56.420] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000163)  
Fostering Torture-Free

**[00:14:58.340] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000164)  
Facilities Initiative,

**[00:15:00.000] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000165)  
The Commission focuses on detention facilities with highest incidences of torture

**[00:15:05.240] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000166)  
Under this program, the CHR conducted 136 visits across 24 facilities in 2025

**[00:15:13.740] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000167)  
and 16 visits across 12 facilities in the first semester of 2026

**[00:15:19.660] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000168)  
The CHR also regularly provides assistance to persons deprived of liberty

**[00:15:26.300] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000169)  
2,503 PDLs were assisted in 2025

**[00:15:31.160] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000170)  
while 1,875 PDLs have been assisted for the first semester of 2026

**[00:15:39.840] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000171)  
The Commission's current financial and human resources for its POD visitation program

**[00:15:46.920] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000172)  
allows it to visit, on average, only one-third of the recorded 2,920 detention facilities

**[00:15:56.280] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000173)  
and, at most, one visit annually

**[00:15:59.320] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000174)  
This is precisely why the Commission welcomes the DBM's grant of P16,019,000

**[00:16:08.300] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000175)  
to expand the POD visitation program of the Commission

**[00:16:12.140] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000176)  
This will allow us to reach more facilities, identify risks earlier,

**[00:16:17.980] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000177)  
recommend corrective measures, and help prevent human rights violations before they occur

**[00:16:22.360] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000178)  
The Commission works

**[00:16:27.090] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000179)  
The Commission's work

**[00:16:28.870] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000180)  
is not limited to investigating human rights violations after they happen

**[00:16:37.150] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000181)  
The CHR has the constitutional mandate to establish a continuing program

**[00:16:42.070] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000182)  
of research, education, and information to enhance respect for the primacy of human rights

**[00:16:49.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000183)  
Through its Human Rights Institute and the Lakbay Karapatan Tungo sa Kamalayan or LAKARAN program

**[00:16:56.730] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000184)  
and other information campaigns,

**[00:16:58.870] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000185)  
the CHR has implemented 1,761 and served 122,950 clients in 2025

**[00:17:10.690] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000186)  
For the first semester of 2026, the CHR has conducted 823 activities,

**[00:17:18.530] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000187)  
reaching 51,716 clients for this year

**[00:17:23.930] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000188)  
Under the policy advisory services.

**[00:17:28.870] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000189)  
So,

**[00:17:29.870] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000190)  
under the Constitution, the CHR is also tasked with monitoring Philippine government's compliance

**[00:17:36.630] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000191)  
with international treaty obligations on human rights

**[00:17:39.650] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000192)  
and providing the government with policy guidance on human rights

**[00:17:43.830] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000193)  
The CHR fulfills this mandate by issuing position papers, policy advisories, and situation reports

**[00:17:52.210] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000194)  
For 2025, the CHR has released 127 issuances,

**[00:17:58.870] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000196)  
while 94 have already been issued for the first semester of 2026

**[00:18:04.110] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000197)  
So we are still on track as to our accomplishment

**[00:18:10.070] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000198)  
The CHR also regularly conducts community-based dialogues or CBDs with rights holders

**[00:18:17.210] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000199)  
to address localized human rights issues

**[00:18:20.370] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000200)  
For 2025, a total of 34 CBDs have been conducted,

**[00:18:25.590] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000201)  
while 16 CBDs have been organized,

**[00:18:28.870] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000202)  
for the first semester of 2026

**[00:18:31.670] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000203)  
We also regularly issue press statements to highlight pressing or urgent human rights concerns,

**[00:18:39.190] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000204)  
laud government agencies' best practices and human rights,

**[00:18:43.550] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000205)  
and to celebrate key international and domestic human rights milestones

**[00:18:48.370] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000206)  
For 2025, the CHR has issued 142 press statements

**[00:18:54.830] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000207)  
and 160 Obligasyon at Karapatan, or

**[00:18:58.710] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000211)  
OK Yan sa Human Rights social media cards.

**[00:19:04.150] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000212)  
To this end, the CHR respectfully forwards its proposed activities for support under Tier 2.

**[00:19:13.670] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000213)  
Allow me to quickly go over them, ranked by priority.

**[00:19:17.990] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000214)  
So for the CHR prioritizes the enhancement of its productivity to ensure efficient delivery of services

**[00:19:26.970] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000215)  
and requests further support in the amount of 108.237 million

**[00:19:34.090] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000216)  
for the implementation of its expanded Places of Deprivation of Liberty Visitation Program and Torture Prevention.

**[00:19:43.910] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000217)  
This also includes support for augmenting mandatory expenses in the central and regional offices.

**[00:19:50.810] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000218)  
This priority is in line with the current administration's trust on digital transformation.

**[00:19:55.290] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000219)  
For Priority Numbers 2 and 3, the Human Rights Action Center and Human Rights Cities,

**[00:20:06.200] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000220)  
we are also seeking support to implement capacity building activities amounting to 18.416 million

**[00:20:16.640] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000221)  
for external clients, specifically for Human Rights Action Center officers and local government units

**[00:20:25.000] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000222)  
towards the development of human rights cities.

**[00:20:28.240] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000223)  
and strengthening human rights based approaches to governance.

**[00:20:32.400] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000224)  
This will support the mainstreaming of human rights principles and services at the barangay level

**[00:20:37.600] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000225)  
and support the implementation of the joint memorandums circular between the DILG and the Commission on Human Rights.

**[00:20:44.580] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000226)  
We also propose investments in our information systems for a total of 10.114 million

**[00:20:51.680] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000227)  
for the continued support and maintenance of the CHR's current digital case management sistem

**[00:20:57.820] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000228)  
and general administration systems.

**[00:21:01.480] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000229)  
Continuing from the last slides,

**[00:21:04.340] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000230)  
the next priority items are the following.

**[00:21:07.960] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000231)  
Priority number 9 and 10

**[00:21:11.600] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000232)  
focus on CHR's role in regional human rights mechanism.

**[00:21:16.720] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000233)  
We appeal to the Honorable Committee

**[00:21:18.760] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000234)  
for support in the proposed amount of 1.934 million

**[00:21:22.920] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000235)  
in hosting fellow Southeast Asian National Human Rights Institution

**[00:21:27.500] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000236)  
for technical and high-level meetings

**[00:21:30.180] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000237)  
to address shared human rights concerns and interests within the ASEAN.

**[00:21:35.760] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000238)  
And we are expecting KASIPO to lead

**[00:21:38.660] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000239)  
the Southeast Asian National Human Rights Institution for next year.

**[00:21:46.200] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000240)  
Numbers 20, 22, and 23

**[00:21:49.880] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000241)  
request augmentation of human rights resources and wages

**[00:21:55.300] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000242)  
with specific focus on

**[00:21:57.480] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000243)  
additional ICT personnel

**[00:21:59.180] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000244)  
for a total of 195.523 million pesos.

**[00:22:04.820] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000245)  
A substantial component of our proposal concerns

**[00:22:09.020] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000246)  
infrastructure as indicated in Numbers 16, 17, and 18

**[00:22:14.080] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000247)  
specifically for infrastructure development

**[00:22:17.800] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000248)  
for our central and regional offices.

**[00:22:21.040] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000249)  
And this includes the construction of a permanent building

**[00:22:23.880] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000250)  
and land acquisition for the CHR Central Office.

**[00:22:27.480] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000251)  
Numbers 8, 13, 15, and 21

**[00:22:32.020] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000252)  
of the Tier 2 proposal

**[00:22:34.240] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000253)  
are meant to strengthen the CHR's operations protection mandate

**[00:22:41.620] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000254)  
and legal services.

**[00:22:43.480] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000255)  
This includes bolstering the CHR's legal clinic caravan

**[00:22:46.980] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000256)  
and revising the current rules of procedure.

**[00:22:51.500] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000257)  
Numbers 7, 11, and 19

**[00:22:54.440] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000258)  
of the Tier 2 proposal

**[00:22:56.120] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000259)  
focus on strengthening human rights education

**[00:22:59.040] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000260)  
and research in the Philippines.

**[00:23:02.180] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000261)  
And Numbers 12 and 14

**[00:23:04.560] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000262)  
of the Tier 2 proposal

**[00:23:07.060] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000263)  
delve into specific human rights concerns

**[00:23:10.280] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000264)  
such as labor-related human rights violations

**[00:23:13.040] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000265)  
and food security.

**[00:23:15.960] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000266)  
To this end, the CHR respectfully appeals

**[00:23:19.400] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000267)  
to the Honorable Committee to reconsider

**[00:23:21.720] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000268)  
the Commission's proposed budget

**[00:23:24.300] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000269)  
to enable the CHR's

**[00:23:26.120] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000270)  
to effectively discharge its mandate,

**[00:23:29.000] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000271)  
strengthen its services,

**[00:23:30.300] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000272)  
and expand its reach to communities

**[00:23:32.300] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000273)  
at the grassroots level.

**[00:23:34.300] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000274)  
Again, we thank the Honorable Chairperson

**[00:23:36.860] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000275)  
and distinguished members of the Committee on Appropriations

**[00:23:40.100] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000276)  
and representatives for their time and attention.

**[00:23:43.780] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000277)  
We also thank Representative Tina Talavera-Lopez

**[00:23:46.680] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000278)  
for sponsoring our budget and lending her voice

**[00:23:49.920] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000279)  
to the cause of strengthening the Philippines'

**[00:23:51.800] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000280)  
National Human Rights Institution,

**[00:23:54.240] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000281)  
which is the Commission on Human Rights.

**[00:23:56.120] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000282)  
Bilang tinalagang National Human Rights Institution ng bansa,

**[00:24:00.940] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000283)  
ang Komisyon ang nagsisilbing watchdog

**[00:24:04.100] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000284)  
at tagapagpayo o advisor ng Estado

**[00:24:06.980] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000285)  
upang magampanan nito ang obligasyong galangin

**[00:24:09.940] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000286)  
at protektahan at itaguyod ang karapatang pantao ng bawat Pilipino.

**[00:24:15.940] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000287)  
The Commission stands ready to answer questions

**[00:24:19.440] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000288)  
of the Honorable Committee

**[00:24:20.640] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000289)  
and provide additional information

**[00:24:23.060] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000290)  
to assist in the deliberation.

**[00:24:24.780] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000291)  
Maraming salamat po at muli magandang hapon po.

**[00:24:26.120] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000292)  
Thank you very much, Chairperson Richard Palpal-latoc.

**[00:24:32.520] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000293)  
Napakaliwanag po ng inyong presentation.

**[00:24:35.820] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000294)  
Very detailed.

**[00:24:37.960] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000295)  
So, let's continue po with our interpellation and discussion.

**[00:24:42.620] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000296)  
And before that, may I remind our distinguished colleagues

**[00:24:46.140] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000297)  
that each member is allotted only five minutes.

**[00:24:49.840] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000298)  
Pero dahil kayo po ay special sa akin,

**[00:24:53.680] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000299)

**[00:24:57.460] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000300)  
ano po...

**[00:24:58.460] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000301)  
For questions or interpellation,

**[00:25:01.640] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000302)  
excluding the responses of the resource persons,

**[00:25:05.400] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000303)  
when the bell sounds,

**[00:25:07.800] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000304)

**[00:25:09.160] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000305)

**[00:25:16.920] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000307)  
Consistent with the long-standing practice of the House,

**[00:25:20.360] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000308)  
the Chair shall recognize minority,

**[00:25:23.480] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000309)

**[00:25:26.940] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000310)  
and depending...

**[00:25:27.460] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000311)  
and majority members in an alternating manner,

**[00:25:31.500] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000312)  
when predictable.

**[00:25:33.080] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000313)  
When...

**[00:25:33.580] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000314)  
The Chair first recognizes the honorable

**[00:25:37.380] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000315)  
Deputy Minority Leader,

**[00:25:41.420] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000316)  
Representative Perci Cendaña,

**[00:25:43.760] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000317)  
of Akbayan Party-list.

**[00:25:46.260] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000318)  
Marami pong salamat, Madam Chair,

**[00:25:48.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000319)  
at magandang hapon po sa CHR family,

**[00:25:51.300] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000320)  
led by Chairperson Papalatok.

**[00:25:53.020] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000321)  
Sa ating mga commissioners,

**[00:25:54.820] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000322)  
at sa ating executive director.

**[00:25:57.460] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000323)  
Looking at your budget,

**[00:26:02.760] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000324)  
isa lang po yung conclusion na pwede nating sabihin.

**[00:26:06.160] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000325)  
Lagi pong sinasabi namin dito sa Kongreso,

**[00:26:08.420] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000326)  
ang budget ang pinaka-concrete expression of priority.

**[00:26:13.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000327)  
And in this case,

**[00:26:15.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000328)  
given the massive cut in the proposed,

**[00:26:19.160] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000329)

**[00:26:23.800] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000330)

**[00:26:27.460] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000331)  
Ang human rights.

**[00:26:29.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000332)  
Sa isang panahon na ina-assail ang human rights,

**[00:26:33.860] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000333)  
hindi po natin makita yung pagbibigay-prioridad.

**[00:26:36.640] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000334)  
Batay po sa binigay na budget sa Commission on Human Rights.

**[00:26:41.480] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000335)  
So, halimbawa po,

**[00:26:44.240] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000336)  
talaga pong tayo ay nababahala

**[00:26:46.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000337)

**[00:26:49.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000338)

**[00:26:51.460] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000339)  
payment of wages of job order

**[00:26:54.540] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000340)  
for the central and regional offices

**[00:26:56.800] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000341)  
of the CHR.

**[00:26:58.500] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000342)

**[00:27:00.100] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000343)  
kung sakali po na hindi natin maisama

**[00:27:02.780] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000344)

**[00:27:05.660] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000345)  
ay hindi mababayaran

**[00:27:07.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000346)

**[00:27:08.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000347)

**[00:27:18.460] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000349)

**[00:27:27.540] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000352)  
Okay po.

**[00:27:28.480] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000353)  
So, to...

**[00:27:29.800] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000354)  
Upang sumusunod,

**[00:27:30.320] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000355)  
sumagot po sa akin yung katanungan,

But definitely,

**[00:29:06.520] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000415)

**[00:29:09.440] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000418)

**[00:29:13.180] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000421)

**[00:29:14.420] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000422)  
ng Madam Chair

**[00:29:15.300] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000423)  
ng CHR Family

**[00:29:16.440] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000424)  
doon sa ating trabaho

**[00:29:18.300] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000425)  
para isulong

**[00:29:19.740] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000426)  
at ipaglaban

**[00:29:20.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000427)  
at protektahan

**[00:29:21.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000428)  
ng karapatang pantaon

**[00:29:22.260] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000429)  
ng ating po mga kababayan.

**[00:29:23.980] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000430)  
Pero bilang mga manggagawa po

**[00:29:25.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000431)  
sa loob ng Hensha,

**[00:29:26.120] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000432)  
dapat protektahan din po natin

**[00:29:27.580] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000433)  
yung kanilang po karapatan

**[00:29:28.900] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000434)  
sa isang humane

**[00:29:29.740] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000435)  
ah,

**[00:29:30.260] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000436)  
workspace.

**[00:29:30.980] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000437)  
So, kaya po,

**[00:29:31.640] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000438)  
ah, Madam Chair,

**[00:29:32.720] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000439)  
at the proper time,

**[00:29:33.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000440)  
we'll make the necessary motion

**[00:29:34.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000441)  
to restore

**[00:29:35.960] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000442)  
the capital outlay proposal

**[00:29:37.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000443)  
of the Commission

**[00:29:38.760] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000444)  
on Human Rights.

**[00:29:39.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000445)  
Let me go to

**[00:29:40.300] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000446)  
more thematic questions.

**[00:29:42.160] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000447)  
Number one po,

**[00:29:43.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000448)  
ah,

**[00:29:43.580] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000449)  
nung isang taon po

**[00:29:44.180] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000450)  
nag-commit ang CHR

**[00:29:45.480] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000451)  
to take the lead

**[00:29:46.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000452)  
in advocating

**[00:29:47.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000453)  
ah,

**[00:29:48.100] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000454)  
for our re-engagement

**[00:29:49.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000455)  
with the ICC.

**[00:29:50.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000456)  
Ano na po ang status

**[00:29:52.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000457)  
nitong effort na ito?

**[00:29:53.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000458)  
At ganoon na po tayo

**[00:29:54.060] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000459)  
kalayo

**[00:29:54.660] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000460)  
o kalapit

**[00:29:55.640] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000461)  
sa muli nating

**[00:29:56.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000462)  
pagsali sa ICC?

**[00:30:00.980] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000463)  
Madam Chair, if I may be allowed.

**[00:30:06.020] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000464)  
Yes, you are recognized, Chairperson Richard.

**[00:30:09.960] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000465)  
Tama po. Patuloy pa rin naman po ang aming stand and advocacy for the rejoining of our country sa International Criminal Court or the Rome Statute.

**[00:30:24.700] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000466)  
So, katuwang po nito, ang ginagawa po ng komisyon ay continuous po ang ating pag-issue ng press statement and public pronouncements,

**[00:30:37.740] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000467)  
development and dissemination of infographics or IEC materials, media engagement and public information activities,

**[00:30:46.660] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000468)  
and use of online platforms to consolidate ICC-related information.

**[00:30:51.820] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000469)  
Hindi po kasi parte ang komisyon.

**[00:30:54.700] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000470)  
Sa treaty negotiations or informal decisions on ICC, ang role po natin ay monitor, human rights monitor,

**[00:31:07.080] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000471)  
meaning we can only send recommendations to the concerned agencies of the government upang makatulong sa advocacy na pag-rejoin sa ICC.

**[00:31:20.200] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000472)  
Ngunit, katulad po ng nasabi ko,

**[00:31:24.360] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000473)  
ang komisyon,

**[00:31:24.700] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000474)  
ang komisyon po ay patuloy at consistent po sa kanya stand na ang Pilipinas po ay mas nararapat na sumali ulit sa ICC.

**[00:31:35.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000475)  
Madam Chair, meron po ba tayong formal recommendation, policy paper, advisory that we have addressed to various duty bearers reflecting this position and this recommendation?

**[00:31:47.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000476)  
At kung meron po, sino-sino mga duty bearers po yung pinagpaabutan natin na

**[00:31:54.700] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000477)  
mga policy recommendations na ito?

**[00:31:58.700] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000478)  
Madam Chair, meron po.

**[00:32:02.840] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000479)  
Meron po tayo na isyong eight press statements related sa ICC, including the effects of Philippines withdrawal from the ICC,

**[00:32:18.140] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000480)  
the government's decision not to rejoin the ICC,

**[00:32:21.100] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000481)  
the ICC's pre-trial chamber's authorization to proceed with investigation,

**[00:32:26.780] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000482)  
denial of the Philippine government's appeal to suspend the ICC probe and the issuance of warrant of arrest by the ICC in relation to the alleged crime against humanity involving the former president.

**[00:32:40.280] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000483)  
So, then we also issued an EJK report which has been forwarded to all national government agencies including the DOJ, Philippine National Police, the PDEA, and others.

**[00:32:57.100] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000486)  
Madam Chair, marami pong salamat sa ating chairperson ng CHR.

**[00:33:02.120] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000487)  
This is a related point po to the earlier question.

**[00:33:06.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000488)  
Ngayon po, mahalagang turning point.

**[00:33:08.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000489)  
Sa human rights discourse sa Pilipinas, ang paglilitis kay former President Rodrigo Duterte.

**[00:33:15.900] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000490)  
But narratives online and offline tell you otherwise.

**[00:33:20.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000491)  
Patuloy po ang pambababoy, pambabalahura, at pagdismiss sa mga biktima ng drug war.

**[00:33:26.780] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000492)  
Ano po ba kung meron man ang efforts ng CHR para labanan ang ganito po mga narratives?

**[00:33:37.780] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000493)  
Regarding extrajudicial killing, EJK in relation to the drug war, patuloy po yung investigation ng komisyon.

**[00:33:46.900] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000494)  
Nabanggit ko po ng mga nakaraang mga hearings on budget deliberation ng Commission on Human Rights.

**[00:33:53.080] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000495)  
Yung mga efforts na ginawa ng komisyon sa investigasyon at mga problema na na-encounter.

**[00:34:01.160] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000496)  
At sa ngayon, patuloy pa rin po namin binabalikan yung mga kaso upang mabuo at ultimately matapos na po yung ating investigation tungkol dito.

**[00:34:14.380] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000497)  
Gayun din po, patuloy pa rin naman po yung ating assistance sa mga victims or families of victims.

**[00:34:26.280] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000498)  
Isa po sa mga accomplishment ng komisyon patungkol po dito,

**[00:34:33.020] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000499)  
So, eto po, may kinreate po kasing task force ang komisyon na nag-review, nag-re-evaluate, nag-reopen ng mga cases.

**[00:34:56.440] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000500)  
Totaling to 493 reinvestigated cases.

**[00:35:06.550] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000501)  
140 cases na po yung for disposition nitong task force na ito.

**[00:35:12.130] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000502)  
Madam Chair, while we appreciate...

**[00:35:13.670] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000503)  
We appreciate the efforts of the CHR in the reopening of these cases.

**[00:35:17.970] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000504)  
Napapansin lang po kasi ng marami, particular po yung nasa community,

**[00:35:21.750] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000505)  
that CHR's voice is notably absent from the public discourse surrounding the ICC proceedings of former President Rodrigo Duterte.

**[00:35:30.730] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000506)  
Siguro po, humble appeal to the CHR.

**[00:35:33.650] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000507)  
Yung drug war victims po and their families deserve to know that the CHR has always stood and will stand with them in their pursuit of justice.

**[00:35:42.270] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000508)  
Kung naging very vocal po tayo on the issue of rejoining the ICC,

**[00:35:47.170] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000509)  
sana ganun rin po tayo, ganun ka-vocal sa pag-suporta doon sa mga biktima po ng EJK

**[00:35:52.030] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000510)  
na ngayon po ay patuloy na binababoy sa daang mga masasamang puwersa habang ongoing po ang trial ni former President Rodrigo Duterte.

**[00:36:02.950] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000511)  
I cannot overemphasize the role of CHR in reshaping public discourse on human rights.

**[00:36:07.970] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000512)  
Alam po natin, tayo ay nasa panahon ng recovery sa ngayon.

**[00:36:11.710] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000513)  
Sa mahabang panahon po, na-demonize ang human rights sa Pilipinas.

**[00:36:16.150] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000514)  
At may pagkakatoon po tayo ngayong panahon ito para itama yung pagkakamaling iyon.

**[00:36:21.170] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000515)  
And we hope that we start that with rectifying these false narratives na ngayon po ay bastalong lumalaganap because of the ongoing ICC trial of former President Duterte.

**[00:36:35.330] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000516)  
Let me move to the last theme of my questions for this afternoon.

**[00:36:40.050] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000517)  
The CHR previously announced its intention to strengthen work on the relationship between corruption and human rights.

**[00:36:48.370] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000518)  
Nag-adapt na po ba tayo ng policies, investigative framework, or strategic plans para i-frame ang corruption bilang isang human rights concern?

**[00:36:57.550] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000519)  
Yes, Madam Chair. Mayroon na pong ginawang activities and policies on ang Commission.

**[00:37:04.010] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000520)  
In fact, we conducted two public symposiums on corruption.

**[00:37:10.050] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000521)  
One on corruption and human rights upang alamin yung interrelation ng corruption and its effect on human rights.

**[00:37:20.670] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000522)  
Ito po ay ginawa ng Commission at in attendance po dito ay some government agencies and civil society organizations as well as, of course, Your Honor, umattend po kayo doon under the focal chief of Commissioner Beda A. Epres.

**[00:37:40.050] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000523)  
And one activity is conducted by Commissioner Maria Amifaith S. Fider-Reyes.

**[00:37:48.370] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000524)  
Madam Chair, thank you very much again for the efforts of the Commission in this area.

**[00:37:53.390] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000525)  
At siguro po, mahalagang tanungin, para po may pagpatuloy yung trabaho natin dito sa pagbalangkas sa usapin ng korupsyon bilang isang human rights violation at human rights concern, ano po ba yung kailangan resources ng CHR para dito?

**[00:38:10.690] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000526)  
Yun pong...

**[00:38:11.810] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000527)  
Siguro po, Madam Chair, ibabalik ko lamang po doon sa aming tier 2 request or proposals pagkat ito po ay makakatulong sa pagtupad ng Commission sa kanyang mandato.

**[00:38:23.930] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000528)  
Thank you very much, Madam Chair.

**[00:38:25.750] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000529)  
The government and the public must recognize that corruption is a human rights concern.

**[00:38:31.330] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000530)  
Sa bawat piso na pupunta sa bulsa ng iilan, maraming karapatang pantao ang nilalapastangan.

**[00:38:37.490] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000531)

**[00:38:41.810] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000532)

**[00:38:54.670] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000533)

**[00:39:07.710] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000534)

**[00:50:52.560] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000699)

**[00:50:58.560] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000700)

**[00:51:03.780] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000701)

**[00:51:10.680] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000702)

**[00:51:13.460] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000703)

**[00:51:17.600] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000704)

**[00:51:22.080] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000705)

**[00:51:29.180] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000706)

**[00:51:32.720] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000707)  
Barangay

**[00:51:33.780] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000708)  
Human Rights Action Centers in line with the Joint Memorandum Circular of the

**[00:51:39.540] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000709)  
DILG and the CHR.

**[00:51:49.680] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000711)

**[00:51:54.820] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000712)

**[00:52:01.840] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000713)

**[00:52:03.780] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000714)

**[00:52:45.940] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000721)

**[00:52:54.760] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000722)  
and interface with vulnerable sectors.

**[00:52:58.220] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000723)  
For the promotion, human rights promotion po, we conducted lakaran or lakbayangin.

**[00:53:03.380] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000724)  
karapatan tungo sa kamalayan, which is

**[00:53:05.340] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000725)

**[00:53:07.300] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000726)  
activity. So

**[00:53:09.340] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000727)  
we localize, tulad po na

**[00:53:11.420] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000728)

**[00:53:13.140] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000729)

**[00:53:15.160] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000730)

**[00:53:19.040] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-000732)

**[01:11:49.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001272)

**[01:11:52.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001273)  
places of deprivation of liberty.

**[01:11:55.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001274)  
So tama po no,

**[01:11:56.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001275)  
dahil po sa yung limited budget,

**[01:11:58.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001276)  
kalakhan,

**[01:11:59.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001277)  
ang bisita ay sinasabay na rin

**[01:12:01.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001278)
