**[01:12:02.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001279)  
Hindi rin po nagagawa yung

**[01:12:04.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001280)  
supposedly no mandate na

**[01:12:06.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001281)  
random ang visits.

**[01:12:08.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001282)  
So siyempre,

**[01:12:09.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001283)  
dapat doon po natin makikita yung

**[01:12:11.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001284)  
candid picture kung kamusta po yung

**[01:12:14.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001285)  
observation respect of human rights.

**[01:12:16.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001286)  
Pero dahil may in-identify po,

**[01:12:18.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001287)  
dahil may investigation na mangyayari,

**[01:12:20.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001288)  
parang nahahanda.

**[01:12:21.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001289)  
Tama po ba?

**[01:12:24.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001290)  
Ililinawin ko lamang po yung konteksto.

**[01:12:26.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001291)  
Kasi po ang komisyon po ay may

**[01:12:29.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001292)  
mandato mag-investiga

**[01:12:31.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001293)  
ng human rights violation.

**[01:12:32.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001294)  
Ito po yung mga kaso

**[01:12:34.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001295)  
or complaints of human rights violations.

**[01:12:38.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001296)  
Isa po yung prevention,

**[01:12:41.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001297)  
yung jail visitation.

**[01:12:43.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001298)  
Ang sinasabi ko po na isinasabay

**[01:12:46.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001299)  
ay dahil,

**[01:12:47.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001300)  
limited po yung budget,

**[01:12:49.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001301)  
lalo na ng regional offices.

**[01:12:51.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001302)  
Kapag po mayroon silang iniimbestigahan

**[01:12:53.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001303)  
sa isang lugar,

**[01:12:54.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001304)  
halimbawa sa isang probinsya,

**[01:12:56.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001305)  
kung ano po yung madadaanan nila

**[01:12:58.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001306)  
mga places of deprivation of liberty

**[01:13:01.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001307)  
along the way,

**[01:13:02.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001308)  
bibisitahin na po nila.

**[01:13:04.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001309)  
So para matupad pa rin po namin

**[01:13:09.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001310)  
yung aming mandato sa jail visitation.

**[01:13:12.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001311)  
Pero kung mabibigyan po kasi ng

**[01:13:14.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001312)  
additional budget dedicated for jail visitation,

**[01:13:16.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001313)  
mas makakatutok po ang komisyon

**[01:13:20.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001314)  
sa kanyang mandato sa jail visitation.

**[01:13:24.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001315)  
At tama po kayo,

**[01:13:26.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001316)  
nabanggit nyo kanina yung

**[01:13:27.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001317)  
stratehiya na kailangan ay mas madalas

**[01:13:30.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001318)  
at hindi ito announced or

**[01:13:33.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001319)  
uncoordinated yung pagbisita

**[01:13:36.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001320)  
upang malaman natin yung totoong sitwasyon.

**[01:13:39.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001321)  
Yun po yung idea,

**[01:13:41.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001322)  
ang dahilan kung bakit namin

**[01:13:43.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001323)  
itinatagi yung aming torture-free facilities.

**[01:13:46.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001324)  
Kung saan in-identify po na ng komisyon,

**[01:13:49.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001325)  
ano-ano ba yung mga PODs na ito

**[01:13:52.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001326)  
na merong report na merong high incidence

**[01:13:54.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001327)  
of torture or inhuman treatment.

**[01:13:58.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001328)  
Para ito yung bibisitahin regularly

**[01:14:01.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001329)  
and more frequent ng CHR,

**[01:14:03.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001330)  
kasi po ang binabago natin ay kultura.

**[01:14:06.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001331)  
Kultura sa isang place of deprivation of liberty.

**[01:14:10.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001332)  
At hindi po mababago yan sa isang visit lamang.

**[01:14:13.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001333)  
Kundi dapat mas madalas at hindi alam kung saan

**[01:14:14.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001334)  
mas madalas at hindi alam kung kailan ka darating

**[01:14:17.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001335)  
upang magbago yung kanilang kultura.

**[01:14:23.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001336)  
Kung paano nila tinatrato ang isang person deprived of liberty.

**[01:14:29.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001337)  
Maaari po bang makahingi ng kopya

**[01:14:32.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001338)  
at ibigay po sa committee

**[01:14:35.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001339)  
yung sinasabi po na,

**[01:14:37.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001340)  
well, siguro report tungkol sa mga napansing mga facilities

**[01:14:42.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001341)  
na mas frequent.

**[01:14:44.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001342)  
Yung human rights violations,

**[01:14:46.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001343)  
yung incidence of torture.

**[01:14:49.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001344)  
At syempre gusto natin na hindi na ito maulit

**[01:14:51.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001345)  
and through the budget nga na maibibigay ay magawa yan.

**[01:14:55.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001346)  
Kaya po ba yun, Mr. Chair?

**[01:14:56.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001347)  
Yes. Yes, Madam Chair.

**[01:14:58.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001348)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:15:00.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001349)  
Important po na magawa po ng CHR yung mandate niya at syempre in support po tayo sa pagtaas ng budget.

**[01:15:05.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001350)  
Lalo na po itong number one dahil ito po yung pinag-uusapan natin ngayon, di ba po?

**[01:15:11.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001351)  
Kamusta po ba yung mga bahay pag-asa para maalagaan yung mga kapakanan ng kabataan?

**[01:15:16.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001352)  
Paano po ba natin may ensure ng ating mga PDLs ay hindi po unduly deprived of their human rights?

**[01:15:24.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001353)  
Kailangan po, nagagawa ng CHR yung mandato sa pag-check

**[01:15:28.440] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001354)  
and through the means and measures na mas nagagawa at hindi ito, as you said niya po, uncoordinated.

**[01:15:36.780] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001355)  
My next question is in relation to the 2025 National Inquiry of the CHR on red-tagging.

**[01:15:48.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001356)  
So this representation welcomes the executive summary na nirelease po ng CHR

**[01:15:56.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001357)  
na

**[01:15:57.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001358)  
report niya po that it remains an entrenched systemic practice that acts as a precursor to physical harassment,

**[01:16:05.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001359)  
arbitrary detention, and extrajudicial killings.

**[01:16:08.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001360)  
And meron pong preliminary recommendations ang CHR included po doon yung criminalizing yung red-tagging

**[01:16:14.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001361)  
which this representation has filed a bill for.

**[01:16:18.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001362)  
So we eagerly await the full report but habang hinihintay po natin yun,

**[01:16:24.720] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001363)  
gusto po natin itanong sa commission.

**[01:16:27.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001364)  
Given that the findings say that red-tagging acts as an immediate precursor to arbitrary detention and extrajudicial killings,

**[01:16:37.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001365)  
how much po sa MOOE ninyo, kung meron man,

**[01:16:40.660] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001366)  
ang directly allocated to operationalized rapid response legal deployment teams,

**[01:16:46.780] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001367)  
especially for rural and conflict-afflicted areas where access to counsel is severely limited?

**[01:16:53.940] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001368)  
Hingi lang po ako ng permiso, Madam Chair, to check our...

**[01:16:58.800] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001369)  
Wala po kasi kaming budget dedicated for red-tagging.

**[01:17:06.280] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001370)  
For it right now. Dahil bago rin po yung...

**[01:17:08.440] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001371)  
Opo. Naka-lump po siya sa aming protection service.

**[01:17:13.640] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001372)  
Ang total po kasi ng aming budget, allotment for protection program ay 323,278,000.

**[01:17:28.380] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001373)  
Ito po yung...

**[01:17:30.340] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001374)  
kabuuan ng Human Rights Protection Service.

**[01:17:34.500] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001375)  
So kung yung kabuuan, ibig po sabihin nito ay investigation,

**[01:17:38.920] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001376)  
legal services, legal assistance,

**[01:17:42.940] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001377)  
forensic services,

**[01:17:47.520] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001378)  
witness assistance and protection,

**[01:17:51.690] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001379)  
and then,

**[01:17:53.310] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001380)  
humahati pa po dyan yung budget for the prevention service, yung pong jail visitation.

**[01:17:59.750] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001381)  
So, hindi po ako makapagbibigyan.

**[01:18:01.650] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001382)  
Hindi po ako makapagbibigyan ng exact figure dedicated for this activity na tinatanong po ninyo, Your Honor.

**[01:18:10.910] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001383)  
But, to give you the figure in totality, yun po siya.

**[01:18:18.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001384)  
Protection service. Kabuuan po ito ng protection program ng komisyon.

**[01:18:24.870] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001385)  
And siguro, tanong ko na lang din po, given na may report po tayo,

**[01:18:29.690] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001386)  
and then may recommendations tayo,

**[01:18:31.650] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001387)  
bahagi po ba yung recommendations, yung pagbuuan ng protocols or guidelines,

**[01:18:36.070] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001388)  
kung paano po gamitin yung pondo na ito para po mag-rapid response kung kailangan po?

**[01:18:40.810] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001389)  
Yes po. Kasama po yun sa recommendation and of course, the strategy of the commission.

**[01:18:45.610] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001390)  
Maaari po bang makahingi din po ng kopya ang representation na ito through the committee

**[01:18:50.210] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001391)  
kung nabuo na po yung guidelines?

**[01:18:52.410] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001392)  
Yes. Opo. I-submit po namin yung ito.

**[01:18:56.590] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001393)  
Thank you very much. And siguro, ang final manifestation ko na lang din po,

**[01:19:01.650] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001394)  
dahil red tagging also increasingly operates through digital platforms, doxing, deepfix, and coordinated online campaigns,

**[01:19:09.450] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001395)  
may funding po din sanang allocated and guidelines provided towards digital forensic tools, cyber threat monitoring systems,

**[01:19:17.950] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001396)  
and secure database infrastructure to preserve digital evidence.

**[01:19:22.350] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001397)  
Lalo na po mas nakikita natin yung state-backed vilification.

**[01:19:26.710] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001398)  
And of course, similarly sa pagtingin at pag-alam natin,

**[01:19:31.490] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001399)  
na may mga areas na mas frequent yung human rights violations or even yung incidents of torture.

**[01:19:38.390] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001400)  
I'm sure that is also the case for red tagging.

**[01:19:41.490] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001401)  
For example, may mas high-risk regions like Southern Tagalog, Bicol, Negros, Northern Mindanao, na statistically higher sila.

**[01:19:52.150] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001402)  
So maaari po sana ma-consider yung internal allocation formula na, syempre, gusto natin spread out yung mga,

**[01:20:01.290] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001403)  
puwersa, recurso natin. Pero ang reality ay may mga iilang regions na mas kailangan ng focus.

**[01:20:07.990] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001404)  
Ang dulo naman po nito, Madam Chair, nakikita natin na ang human rights situation sa Pilipinas,

**[01:20:14.590] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001405)  
hindi po natin nakikita yung improvement.

**[01:20:17.990] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001406)  
And so, yung mas dagdag na pondo, suporta sa CHR, at lalo na rin po yung sa pag-capacitate at pag-empower ng mga civil society organizations,

**[01:20:29.790] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001407)  
people's organizations,

**[01:20:31.290] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001408)  
na mapag-aralan nila kung ano yung human rights nila, mapanghawakan, maprotektahan nila nang sama-sama yung instrument and key

**[01:20:38.790] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001409)  
para po malabanan yung systematic violations na nararanasan natin ngayon.

**[01:20:44.790] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001410)  
That's all, Madam Chair. Maraming salamat po. And of course, tuloy-tuloy po sana ang gawain ng CHR.

**[01:20:51.790] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-001411)  
Thank you, Representative Ko, for your manifestation of support, of course.

**[01:20:56.790] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-001412)  
Before we proceed, I would like to recognize

**[01:20:59.790] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-001413)  
our former President, Representative Gloria Macapagal-Arroyo of Pampanga.

**[01:21:05.790] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-001414)  
And also, Representative J. Vergara of Nueva Ecija.

**[01:21:10.790] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-001415)  
Nandito rin po kasama natin si Representative Sara Ilago of Gabriela Partilis.

**[01:21:17.790] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-001416)  
Moving forward po, from majority, I would like to recognize Representative J. Vergara.

**[01:21:27.490] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-001417)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:21:29.490] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-001418)  
Simple lamang po.

**[01:21:31.490] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-001419)  
Magandang hapon po sa inyo, CHR family.

**[01:21:35.490] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-001420)  
In deference to my uncle, who was a chairperson.

**[01:21:42.490] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-001421)  
Ang pangalan po niya ay Atty. Sedfrey Ordoñez.

**[01:21:48.490] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-001422)  
At naging chairperson po ng CHR,

**[01:21:52.490] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-001423)  
nagmamanifest lang po ako ng buong pusong suporta sa inyo. Maraming salamat po.

**[01:21:57.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001424)  
Maraming salamat po, Kong.

**[01:21:59.490] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-001425)  
Thank you. Talagang yan lang po ang ipinunta po dito ni Representative Vergara para sa manifestation of support.

**[01:22:06.490] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-001426)  
Next, from minority, Senior Deputy Minority Leader, Laila de Lima. You are recognized.

**[01:22:14.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001427)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Chair. Good afternoon to everyone. Good afternoon to my colleagues. Good afternoon to the CHR family.

**[01:22:21.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001428)  
Alam niyo, I was looking at the faces and mukhang dalawa o tatlo na lang yung mga familiar faces sa akin.

**[01:22:27.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001429)  
O lima pala.

**[01:22:29.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001430)  
Lima pala because most of them, I think, have retired already and they are really new faces.

**[01:22:35.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001431)  
Including, of course, the chairperson and the commissioners of the commission.

**[01:22:41.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001432)  
Well, some of my questions have been touched already.

**[01:22:45.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001433)  
Although, I came here also to express my full, to manifest my full support for the budget of the CHR

**[01:22:53.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001434)  
and to appeal for the restoration of

**[01:22:56.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001435)  
of whatever it needed to be restored.

**[01:22:59.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001436)  
You're the Tier 2. You're Tier 2 proposals.

**[01:23:03.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001437)  
Para sa akin kasi, this committee must look at the CHR budget

**[01:23:10.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001438)  
not simply as another item in the NEP,

**[01:23:13.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001439)  
but as an investment in the constitutional guarantees that protect every Filipino.

**[01:23:19.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001440)  
And in a democracy, Madam Chair, there can be no more important investment

**[01:23:25.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001441)  
than the protection, promotion, and prevention of human rights.

**[01:23:31.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001442)  
Ang unang tanong ko po, we all know the three core mandates of the CHR.

**[01:23:35.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001443)  
These are the three P's.

**[01:23:37.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001444)  
Prevention, protection, and promotion.

**[01:23:40.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001445)  
Which of these three core mandates that CHR has been experiencing a lot of challenges?

**[01:23:48.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001446)  
In terms of resources and in terms also of implementation of your core programs

**[01:23:54.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001447)  
and activities.

**[01:23:56.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001448)  
And if you're experiencing those challenges, can you tell us why exactly?

**[01:24:02.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001449)  
Madam Chair, thank you for the question.

**[01:24:05.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001450)  
In terms of resources, the most challenging mandate of the Commission would be the prevention service.

**[01:24:14.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001451)  
As I mentioned earlier, the prevention service is sharing budget with the protection service.

**[01:24:22.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001452)  
There was no dedicated allocation for the prevention service

**[01:24:29.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001453)  
except for a small chunk from the protection service.

**[01:24:33.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001454)  
So that's why I was saying earlier that our field investigators would include jail visits

**[01:24:42.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001455)  
when they proceed to conducting investigation in the field.

**[01:24:48.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001456)  
So aside from the investigation,

**[01:24:50.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001457)  
they conduct those visitations?

**[01:24:52.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001458)  
Yes, ma'am.

**[01:24:53.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001459)  
Para makatipid?

**[01:24:54.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001460)  
Para makatipid.

**[01:24:56.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001461)  
What else?

**[01:24:58.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001462)  
In the prevention itself, your cases, are there backlogs?

**[01:25:04.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001463)  
Cases?

**[01:25:05.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001464)  
Yes.

**[01:25:06.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001465)  
We have actually resolved the majority of the case backlogs of the Commission.

**[01:25:13.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001466)  
So from 28,000 cases,

**[01:25:19.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001467)  
from 1987 to 2025,

**[01:25:24.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001468)  
we have resolved 95.8% of the backlog cases.

**[01:25:30.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001469)  
And that would translate to around 27,000 cases concluded by the CHR.

**[01:25:38.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001470)  
So we have a pending case of around 1,200 backlog cases.

**[01:25:48.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001471)  
Okay.

**[01:25:49.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001472)  
What about the drug war killings?

**[01:25:51.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001473)  
What is the status of the investigation of the CHR?

**[01:25:54.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001474)  
I have not been aware of any updates on the status of investigation into drug war killings

**[01:26:02.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001475)  
because I suppose in spite of the ICC,

**[01:26:04.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001476)  
CHR continues to do its own investigation of these drug war killings.

**[01:26:09.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001477)  
And in conjunction especially with the DOJ,

**[01:26:13.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001478)  
yung AO35 na minensyon nyo kanina,

**[01:26:15.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001479)  
it's good to know that you are still supporting

**[01:26:18.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001480)  
that.

**[01:26:19.490] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001481)  
And what exactly is the role of the CHR in the AO35 mechanism?

**[01:26:24.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001482)  
So, thank you Madam Chair, Your Honor.

**[01:26:27.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001483)  
In terms of the drug war cases,

**[01:26:31.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001484)  
majority of the cases when we assumed office have already been concluded.

**[01:26:36.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001485)  
So when we assumed office,

**[01:26:39.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001486)  
we decided to reinvestigate some of the cases

**[01:26:43.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001487)  
because of the challenges experienced by our investigators.

**[01:26:47.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001488)  
During that time.

**[01:26:49.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001489)  
So as of today,

**[01:26:52.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001490)  
there are already 493 cases for reinvestigation.

**[01:27:00.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001491)  
And our task force has concluded 289 cases related to the drug war.

**[01:27:15.500] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001492)  
You mentioned reinvestigation?

**[01:27:17.500] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001493)  
Reinvestigated.

**[01:27:18.500] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001494)  
Because as I mentioned,

**[01:27:19.500] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001495)  
there were challenges encountered by the Commission

**[01:27:23.500] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001496)  
during that time in terms of...

**[01:27:26.500] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001497)  
Those resolved cases have been forwarded already to the DOJ.

**[01:27:30.500] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001498)  
Most of them were dismissed actually

**[01:27:32.500] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001499)  
because of lack of evidence.

**[01:27:35.500] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001500)  
The challenges as I mentioned are

**[01:27:38.500] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001501)  
the hostile environment,

**[01:27:43.500] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001502)  
non-cooperation of the victims or families of victims and witnesses,

**[01:27:48.500] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001503)  
non-provision of documents with the Philippine National Police.

**[01:27:56.180] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001504)  
So...

**[01:27:57.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001505)  
I would note the very low disposition of cases of the drug war killings,

**[01:28:02.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001506)  
even in the DOJ.

**[01:28:04.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001507)  
Only a few are being pursued because of the same reasons or grounds that you just cited.

**[01:28:10.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001508)  
May I know the position of the CHR on the rejoining of the Philippines in ICC?

**[01:28:19.180] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001509)  
It's a consistent position actually, Madam Chair,

**[01:28:22.180] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001510)  
of the Commission on Human Rights for the State to rejoin the ICC.

**[01:28:28.180] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001511)  
It's an additional guarantee or assurance of accountability on human rights violation.

**[01:28:37.180] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001512)  
So, we have been issuing statements to the effect that encourage the State to rejoin the ICC.

**[01:28:48.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001513)  
And only on those special kinds of cases,

**[01:28:51.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001514)  
the crimes against humanity, genocide, etc.

**[01:28:58.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001515)  
So, you have absolutely no reservation about the Philippine government rejoining

**[01:29:03.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001516)  
or Philippines rejoining as a State party to the Rome Statute.

**[01:29:08.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001517)  
As for the NTFL,

**[01:29:10.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001518)  
because red-tagging has been mentioned earlier,

**[01:29:13.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001519)  
is there any oversight function

**[01:29:15.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001520)  
or does the CHR exercise oversight function

**[01:29:18.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001521)  
of the NTFL?

**[01:29:20.180] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001522)  
No, Your Honor.

**[01:29:21.180] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001523)  
We don't have oversight function over the NTFL.

**[01:29:26.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001524)  
And that's not at all possible?

**[01:29:28.180] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001525)  
I don't think so because it's a whole of government approach.

**[01:29:32.180] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001526)  
It's a whole of government approach because we're talking here about government projects.

**[01:29:36.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001527)  
Yes.

**[01:29:37.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001528)  
Although, basically, I do not agree with that,

**[01:29:40.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001529)  
that we should really have NTFL because it should be the government agencies concerned,

**[01:29:46.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001530)  
the relevant government agencies,

**[01:29:47.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001531)  
that should be implementing those projects,

**[01:29:52.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001532)  
those programs and projects.

**[01:29:54.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001533)  
That's why we're talking about red-tagging.

**[01:29:58.180] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001534)  
Yes.

**[01:30:00.000] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001535)  
attributed also to NTF-ELCAC. So in spite of that, you don't have any intention of

**[01:30:06.560] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001536)  
getting it into your work as oversight?

**[01:30:13.600] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001536-split-2)  
Basically, because we don't have oversight

**[01:30:15.940] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001537)  
function over the NTF-ELCAC.

**[01:30:19.000] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001537-split-2)  
Over the NTF-ELCAC, even in your general monitoring and compliance, the government's

**[01:30:26.920] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001538)  
compliance with this treaty obligations, we can go in there?

**[01:30:31.800] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001538-split-2)  
Yes, that's part of the work of the Commission, to monitor the compliance of the government.

**[01:30:40.800] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001539)  
But in terms of oversight, we don't have it because it's under the Office of the President and we are an independent institution.

**[01:30:48.760] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001540)  
So what we can do is to monitor, only to monitor the activities and investigate if there are violations.

**[01:34:41.500] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001583-split-4)  
So, the Commission on Human

**[01:34:44.430] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001584)  
rights was designated as the gender ombud under the magna carta of women so as the gender ombud

**[01:34:51.580] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001585)  
the function of the commission just one one moment

**[01:35:01.950] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001586)  
actually in in in in layman's term as gender ombud we monitor the philippine government's

**[01:35:20.880] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001587)

**[01:35:26.400] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001588)

**[01:35:27.320] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001589)

**[01:35:45.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001590)

**[01:35:59.800] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001591)

**[01:36:01.100] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001591-split-2)  
yes madam chair your honor

**[01:36:03.300] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001592)  
That includes investigating cases of gender-based violations.

**[01:36:08.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001593)

**[01:36:28.980] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001596)  
yes po sapagkat bukod dun sa mga traditional methods of filing complaints

**[01:36:36.600] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001597)  
na walk-in, phone-in or email

**[01:36:40.100] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001598)

kung saan ang isang complainant or mayroong hinain na nalabag ang kanyang karapatan

hindi lamang po ng isang kababaihan kundi ng lahat ng paglabag sa karapatang pantao

ay po pwedeng mag-ha-in ng reklamo sa pamamagitan na itong online platform na ito itong mismo

itong 2026 wala pa po tayo

nung 2026

itong 2026 wala pa po tayo

**[01:38:11.950] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001613)  
meron na po tayo natanggap na 178 cases involving women

**[01:38:19.170] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001614)  
and involving persons of diverse SOGI

**[01:38:23.010] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001615)  
apat po

**[01:38:24.110] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001616)  
of these cases po

**[01:38:27.270] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001617)  
sa 2025, 2026 ilan po dito ang naimbestigahan

**[01:38:32.490] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001618)  
na risolba or nai-refer sa appropriate agency

**[01:38:36.950] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001619)  
at ilan din po ang pending

**[01:38:39.210] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001620)  
Yun po yung bilang ng

**[01:38:41.050] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001621)  
cases investigated.

**[01:38:43.150] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001621-split-2)  
Yung binigay niyo po sa akin na the data, cases investigated.

**[01:38:48.100] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001621-split-3)  
Yes.

**[01:38:48.800] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001621-split-4)  
So dito po sa ano yung disposition po nito, naimbestigahan niyo ilan po yung

**[01:38:52.590] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001622)  
naresolba na diyan

**[01:38:54.110] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001623)  
o nai-refer? Meron po ba tayong tala?

**[01:38:57.810] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001624)  
Wala po kami ngayong data. We will just provide

**[01:39:01.210] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001625)  
yung para ma-accurate po yung aming

**[01:39:02.230] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001626)  
maibigay na data.

**[01:39:04.550] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001626-split-2)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[01:39:05.810] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001627)  
another function

**[01:39:08.050] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001629)  
does your

**[01:39:08.950] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001630)  
function also include

**[01:39:11.050] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001631)  
monitoring government agencies' compliance with laws.

**[01:39:15.470] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001632)  
For instance, the compliance of RA 9710 or the Magna Carta on Women.

**[01:39:21.590] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001633)  
Is that also part of your function?

**[01:39:23.790] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001634)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[01:39:25.210] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001635)  
So, are you closely monitoring the Philippine Commission on Women

**[01:39:32.730] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001636)  
in their monitoring of the compliance of government agencies

**[01:39:39.070] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001637)  
in the requirement of RA 9710?

**[01:39:42.750] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001638)  
Do we have findings or reports about that?

**[01:39:49.250] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001639)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:39:51.810] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001640)  
You're right. We have a connection with the Philippine Commission on Women.

**[01:39:59.430] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001641)  
And aside from that, we also do gender-based violence mapping of local government units.

**[01:40:09.070] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001642)  
And also, the monitoring of Barangay Baudesk, Violence Against Women Desk.

**[01:40:17.710] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001643)  
So, I forgot, I missed the other question.

**[01:40:21.450] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001644)  
Yes. Yung isa po kasing problema natin diyan from the last report that I saw from the Philippine Commission on Women,

**[01:40:29.730] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001645)  
madami pong government agencies ang hindi pa rin compliant doon sa requirement ng RA 9710.

**[01:40:36.230] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001646)  
So, meron po ba tayong ginagawa kapag

**[01:40:39.070] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001647)  
um,

**[01:40:40.470] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001648)  
consistently,

**[01:40:42.150] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001649)  
ang nakikita po kasi natin, consistent yung failure to comply with the requirements of the law.

**[01:40:47.510] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001650)  
Parang taon-taon po talaga hindi nagagawa.

**[01:40:50.730] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001651)  
So ano po ang pwedeng magawa ng CHR diyan?

**[01:40:55.510] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001652)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Chair, Your Honor.

**[01:40:57.470] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001653)  
Ang ginawa po ng Commission at ng PCW dito sa aspetong ito ay

**[01:41:03.170] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001654)  
nag-issue po kami jointly with the PCW

**[01:41:08.610] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001655)  
ng notice po sa mga agencies upang sila ay magpaliwanag kung bakit hindi nila

**[01:41:21.510] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001656)  
nasusunod, natutupad yung requirements ng batas.

**[01:41:27.610] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001657)  
Meron na po bang mga paliwanag? Do we have anything na?

**[01:41:31.850] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001658)  
Ongoing po, kailan po?

**[01:41:35.500] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001658-split-2)  
This year po namin nagawa yun. So siguro ongoing po, wala po kasi akong data sa ngayon.

**[01:41:41.830] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001659)  
Ongoing po yung pasok siguro ng mga compliance ng mga government agencies dito.

**[01:41:48.090] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001660)  
We'll just provide update po kung ano man po yung merong available sa amin sa ngayon.

**[01:41:52.550] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001661)  
Yes po. Siguro ang pakiusap ko na lamang po sa CHR na maging medyo ano po tayo,

**[01:41:58.650] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001662)  
stricto siguro this time kasi antagal-tagal.

**[01:42:01.850] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001663)  
na po nung batas. Hindi po nako-comply talaga yung nakasaad doon.

**[01:42:06.810] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001664)  
Maybe it's about time that maging seryoso po tayo sa paghahabol sa mga ahensya

**[01:42:11.850] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001665)  
to comply with the provisions of the law.

**[01:42:14.530] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001666)  
So my last question would be, Madam Speaker, yung budget cut from your original proposal

**[01:42:22.350] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001667)  
to the NEP level, will that significantly affect the function and role of the CHR

**[01:42:28.490] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001668)  
as the gender ombud?

**[01:42:31.850] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001669)  
Bago ko lang po sagutin, balikan ko lamang po yung kanina una on the compliance of government agencies.

**[01:42:41.310] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001670)  
So ang isa pong strategy na ginawa ng commission ng CHR at ng PCW is we come up with a memorandum of agreement

**[01:42:50.810] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001671)  
para kami po ay we can jointly monitor the compliance of these government agencies under the Magna Carta of Women.

**[01:42:59.970] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001672)  
Ngayon po dun sa inyong...

**[01:43:01.850] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001673)  
sumunod na katanungan, as I said, katulad po ng mga tanong kanina na nauna,

**[01:43:11.420] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001674)  
of course, maapektuhan po yung aming serbisyo dahil sa budget and resources.

**[01:43:19.420] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001675)  
Dahil patuloy, ito po ay regular nang naging problema ng komisyon

**[01:43:23.840] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001676)  
and we learned to adapt, actually, kung paano namin gagamitin yung meager resources of the commission.

**[01:43:30.220] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001677)  
But of course, it would help.

**[01:43:33.800] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001678)  
if the Congress would grant us additional budget for this.

**[01:43:39.740] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001679)  
Especially kung dedicated po, ang budget po ay dedicated for the compliance as the gender ombud,

**[01:43:46.720] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001680)  
mas makakatulong po ng gusto.

**[01:43:49.720] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001681)  
In fact, sa mga previous budget deliberation in previous years po,

**[01:43:57.000] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001682)  
isa po ito sa mga hinahilayit ng komisyon,

**[01:43:59.200] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001683)  
yung mga special laws providing additional mandates.

**[01:44:03.800] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001684)  
to the commission na wala pong additional budget na binibigay.

**[01:44:11.300] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001685)  
Yun po.

**[01:44:11.800] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001686)  
Patuloy pa rin namin hihingin, kung sakali pong, if you would be generous enough to grant the CHR budget

**[01:44:19.080] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001687)  
for these special laws or special mandates provided to us.

**[01:44:24.680] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001688)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Chair.

**[01:44:26.220] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001689)  
Madam Chair, I therefore join our colleagues in their call

**[01:44:31.080] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001690)  
to restore the original proposed budget.

**[01:44:33.740] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001691)  
Maraming maraming salamat po.

**[01:44:35.740] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-001692)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[01:44:37.740] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-001693)  
Thank you, Representative Iris Montes, for your support sa increase pa rin ng CHR budget.

**[01:44:45.740] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-001694)  
Next on line po, from minority, Representative Sara Ilago.

**[01:44:53.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001695)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Chair.

**[01:44:56.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001696)  
Nais kong malaman kung ano ang

**[01:44:58.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001697)  
original

**[01:44:59.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001698)  
proposal

**[01:45:00.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001699)  
at mga nakasama

**[01:45:01.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001700)  
sa NEP na pondo

**[01:45:02.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001701)  
para sa

**[01:45:03.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001702)  
Human Rights Protection Program, Human Rights Promotion Program, at Human Rights Policy Advisory Program.

**[01:45:16.410] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-001703)  
Chairman Papalito, you are recognized.

**[01:45:18.830] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001704)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:45:19.610] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001705)  
So ang budget po na nasama sa NEP for personal service, P718,805,000 yung pong binigay sa NEP.

**[01:45:40.940] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001706)  
For MOOE, P492,454,000.

**[01:45:47.460] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001707)  
And for RLIP,

**[01:45:51.140] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001708)  
61,333,000 or a total of 1,272,000,000.

**[01:45:58.120] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001709)  
Kung ang tinatanong po natin ay yung distribution nito sa mga core services.

**[01:46:03.720] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001710)  
So for majority po kasi ng budget personal service.

**[01:46:13.200] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001711)  
So for the protection, ang allocated budget po ay,

**[01:46:22.620] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001712)  
P379,531,000.

**[01:46:27.280] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001713)  
Then for Human Rights Promotion Program, P90,480,000.

**[01:46:33.500] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001714)  
For Human Rights Policy Program, P79,342,000.

**[01:46:38.800] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001715)  
And then for Jail Visitation, P28,086,000.

**[01:46:47.940] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001716)  
Bahagi po siya ng total ng protection program.

**[01:46:55.050] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001717)  
Maraming salamat sa kasagutan.

**[01:46:57.650] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001718)  
At marami po dito ay napakalayo doon sa original proposal ng CHR

**[01:47:03.630] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001719)  
para magampana ng buong-buo ang kanilang napakahalagang trabaho.

**[01:47:07.390] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001720)  
Kaya po, nais ko pong malaman rin kung yung mga pondo, halimbawa,

**[01:47:14.810] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001721)  
ng mga mahalagang centers natin.

**[01:47:17.050] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001722)  
Halimbawa po yung Child Rights Center, Center for Gender Equality and Women's Human Rights,

**[01:47:23.170] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001723)  
Center for Disability.

**[01:47:25.050] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001724)  
At lahat po ng ating mga centers sa ilalim po ng CHR.

**[01:47:31.390] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001725)  
Paano humaapektuhan ang trabaho ng mga centers na ito?

**[01:47:35.350] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001726)  
At dahil hindi po naibibigay ng buong po yung hinihiling natin na kinakailangan

**[01:47:40.910] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001727)  
para sa pagampan ng atungkulin para sa susunod na taon?

**[01:47:46.250] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001728)  
Unang-una po, iyong pong budget, kung tinatanong niyo po sa ating mga centers,

**[01:47:52.610] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001729)  
nakapaloob po siya doon sa Human Rights Policy Program.

**[01:47:54.690] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001730)  
Sa Human Rights Policy Program po ay dalawa po yung core functions na ginagawa dito.

**[01:48:05.210] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001731)  
Ito po yung Policy Linkages Offices, kung ito yung gumagawa ng monitoring activities

**[01:48:14.370] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001732)  
and international engagement.

**[01:48:20.120] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001733)  
At pangalawa, yung pong center.

**[01:48:21.840] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001734)  
So, naghahati po sila doon sa budget na...

**[01:48:26.160] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001735)  
79,342,000.

**[01:48:29.660] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001736)  
Yung pong mga...

**[01:48:31.820] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001737)  
Sa pondong ito, kaya naman pong tugunan yung mga existing activities and programs.

**[01:48:37.220] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001738)  
Pero, hindi po tayo...

**[01:48:39.440] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001739)  
Hindi po natin kakayani mag-introduce ng bagong mga...

**[01:48:43.620] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001740)  
or expansion ng mga existing activities.

**[01:48:47.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001741)  
Walang expansion, Madam Chair, pero meron po kayong naging proposal.

**[01:48:52.680] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001742)  
Meron po.

**[01:48:53.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001743)  
Para mapalawak.

**[01:48:55.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001744)  
At mapagyaman pa ang kanilang...

**[01:48:56.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001745)  
Inais ko rin pong mabanggit na rin, for the record,

**[01:49:00.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001746)  
ang Center for Disability and Health-Related Rights

**[01:49:03.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001747)  
at ang Center for Social Justice and Humanitarian Protection.

**[01:49:07.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001748)  
Abukad pa po sa napakahalaga na Child Rights Center

**[01:49:09.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001749)  
at Center for Gender Equality and Women's Human Rights.

**[01:49:12.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001750)  
Mahalaga ito, Madam Chair, dahil sa mga gawain din natin dito sa Kongreso.

**[01:49:17.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001751)  
Napakahalagang references.

**[01:49:19.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001752)  
Yung mga nilalabas ko ng mga situation reports,

**[01:49:22.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001753)  
assessment, monitoring, evaluation,

**[01:49:26.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001754)  
policy analysis na nilalabas ng mga centers na ito

**[01:49:32.830] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001755)  
bilang reference sa mga ginagawa natin ng mga panukala dito sa Kongreso.

**[01:49:38.410] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001756)  
Kaya ho, lahat ng maibibigay natin na pagtulak at pagsisika

**[01:49:43.210] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001757)  
para matugunan yung mga hindi na bigyan ng pondo sa NEP.

**[01:49:48.850] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001758)  
At dapat ho gawin ng committee and appropriations na bigyan ng prioridad ang mga ito.

**[01:49:53.310] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001759)  
Ngayon naman, Madam Chair, tungkol ho sa mga naisagawang community,

**[01:49:57.470] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001760)  
mga community dialogues, mga community-based dialogues.

**[01:50:02.490] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001761)  
Ang mga regional at local, ang mga regional offices po ba natin sa buong bansa

**[01:50:07.910] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001762)  
ay nakapagsagawa ng ganitong tipo ng mga dialogues?

**[01:50:12.130] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001763)  
Yes po, Madam Chair, Your Honor.

**[01:50:14.290] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001764)  
Kasama po sa mga regular activities ng ating mga regional offices,

**[01:50:18.850] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001765)  
ang community-based dialogues.

**[01:50:23.250] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001766)  
Ilan po yung mga participants at mga naaabot ng mga dialogues na ito?

**[01:50:27.070] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001767)  
At kung meron din kayong information,

**[01:50:29.970] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001768)  
sa mga NGOs, civil society organizations na naaabot natin pagdating sa mga diyalogo na ito.

**[01:50:37.870] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001769)  
Mahalaga ito, Madam Chair, kasi ito yung magtitiyak na nakalapat sa katotohanan

**[01:50:42.810] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001770)  
kung ano man yung mga programa, proyekto, aktividad na sinasagawa ng CHR,

**[01:50:48.730] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001771)  
lalong-lalo na sa pagtindig para sa karapatang pantao.

**[01:50:52.350] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001772)  
For 2025 po, nakapagsagawa po ang komisyon ng 34 community-based dialogues.

**[01:50:59.970] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001773)  
Sa ngayon po, as of June 2026, mayroon na po tayo nagawang 16 community-based dialogues.

**[01:51:06.950] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001774)  
Hindi po ako makapagbigay ng actual na bilang ng kung ilan yung participants,

**[01:51:12.090] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001775)  
but based on our promotion activities, kasi kasama po sa mga CBDs namin ay promotion activities,

**[01:51:20.590] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001776)  
meron po kaming talang 122,950 individuals who participated in promotion activities.

**[01:51:29.970] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001777)  
In 2025, sa ngayon po, as of June 2026, meron na pong 51,716 individuals.

**[01:51:39.990] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001778)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Chair, at marami tayong naaabot.

**[01:51:43.910] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001779)  
Pero kung itutumbas din natin ito, kung makikita po natin yung ulat ng CHR para sa Human Rights Education Program,

**[01:51:50.450] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001780)  
malaki talaga na limitation yung kulang na pondo pagdating sa pag-abot sa mas malawak pa.

**[01:51:57.270] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001781)  
Kasi bagamat marami, nasa libo na.

**[01:51:59.970] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001782)  
Pero milyong-milyong mga Pilipino pa yung hindi naaabot ng ganitong tipo ng mga programa.

**[01:52:04.910] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001783)  
Kaya nais ko rin malaman kung may pag-uusap ba sa ngayon sa DepEd, sa CHED,

**[01:52:10.270] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001784)  
at sa mga institutions natin na ma-integrate din yung Human Rights Education.

**[01:52:17.810] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001785)  
Alam ko, Madam Chair, may mga panukala dito sa Kongreso para ma-institutionalize ang Human Rights Education,

**[01:52:23.310] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001786)  
pero sa bahagi ngayon ng CHR, meron na ba tayong mga existing na mga partnerships sa iba't ibang institutions

**[01:52:29.610] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001787)  
para mapalakas ang ating Human Rights Education Program?

**[01:52:33.670] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001788)  
Unang-una po, Madam Chair, Your Honor, unang-una po, patuloy po yung ugnayan natin with the Commission on Higher Education

**[01:52:41.930] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001789)  
and DepEd on Human Rights Education, including the review of the curriculum ng mga kurso.

**[01:52:56.750] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001790)  
So, on the part of the...

**[01:52:59.610] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001791)  
On the part of the Commission on Human Rights, yun po palang ugnayan na yun with the DepEd and CHED

**[01:53:05.950] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001792)  
ay kasama po ang may initiative po ang Commission on Human Rights as well as HRBBMC

**[01:53:13.810] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001793)  
para maging part ng curriculum.

**[01:53:18.250] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001794)  
On, of course, for MEMCOM, yung pong martial law, ang CHR naman as the general concept of human rights.

**[01:53:26.850] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001795)  
On the part of...

**[01:53:29.610] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001796)  
On the part of the Commission on Human Rights, we have existing partnership with higher educational institutions all over the Philippines.

**[01:53:40.570] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001797)  
Ang tawag po namin sa kanila ay Center for Human Rights Education.

**[01:53:46.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001798)  
Partnership po ito with this higher educational institution for human rights promotion and research.

**[01:53:55.450] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001799)  
Then, we also have...

**[01:53:57.630] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001800)  
Ongoing discussion with the Philippine Association of Law Schools on the inclusion of...

**[01:54:08.210] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001801)  
Collaboration with the PALS on human rights activities and promotions.

**[01:54:16.750] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001802)  
Madam Chair, hingin na lang po natin yung ulat na tungkol sa extension of services or outreach efforts po natin

**[01:54:27.770] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001803)  
para ma-enjoy pa yung...

**[01:54:29.230] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001804)  
Public at private actors sa human rights education.

**[01:54:32.890] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001805)  
Ngayon naman ho, sa naulat nyo kanina, nasa 800 plus yung cases na lumapit ho sa inyo

**[01:54:38.730] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001806)  
na may kinalaman sa gender-based violence.

**[01:54:41.810] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001807)  
Lahat ho ba ito ay may kinalaman sa state-perpetrated, state-sponsored or condoned

**[01:54:47.270] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001808)  
by any means na type ng violence against women and all genders?

**[01:54:53.630] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001809)  
Hindi po.

**[01:54:54.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001810)  
So, kailangan natin i-disaggregate yung data.

**[01:54:59.230] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001811)  
Kasi, halo po ito, merong state actor or state sanctioned or state agent.

**[01:55:10.030] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001812)  
Meron, karamihan dito actually ay private individuals ang respondents.

**[01:55:16.250] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001813)  
Pero dahil kasama po sa mandato natin na tignan, yung due diligence, compliance ng ating pamahalaan

**[01:55:26.230] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001814)  
on the protection and promotion of human rights.

**[01:55:29.230] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001815)  
At kasama po sa inaimbestigaan ng komisyon ito.

**[01:55:34.330] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001816)  
So, ang objective would be to determine kung sapat po ba yung mga mekanismo at mga solusyon at tugon

**[01:55:42.150] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001817)  
na ginagawaan ng ating pamahalaan sa mga kaso ng paglabag ng karapatan ng kababaihan.

**[01:55:51.150] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001818)  
Madam Chair, kukunin ho natin yung disaggregated data at tungkol dito.

**[01:55:55.090] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001819)  
Pero para sa mga kaso halimbawa na state-perpetrated or state-sponsored,

**[01:56:00.510] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001820)  
lahat ho ba ng mga kaso na lumalapit sa inyo ay nadidispose po natin agad, na-address po natin agad?

**[01:56:07.490] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001821)  
O may mga challenges pa ho?

**[01:56:09.330] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001822)  
Halimbawa, pag natitiyak natin na mapapatawag ang dapat mapatawag,

**[01:56:14.970] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001823)  
nagagawa ho ba ito? At meron bang nananagot?

**[01:56:19.230] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001824)  
Yes po, nagagawa naman po ito at natatapos din agad.

**[01:56:23.650] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001825)  
Although, katulad nung nabanggit ko kanina, Madam Chair, Your Honor,

**[01:56:28.410] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001826)  
tinutukan po ng komisyon yung backlog.

**[01:56:30.510] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001827)  
From 1987 to 2025, more than 20,000 or almost 30,000 cases po ito.

**[01:56:39.630] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001828)  
So, pagkatapos po yan, mas makakatutok na kami sa mga current cases na nasa around 2,942 cases na lamang po tayo.

**[01:56:52.830] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001829)  
So, mas mapapabilis na po yung investigation and conclusion of the investigation of these cases.

**[01:57:00.510] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001830)  
Maraming salamat po sa paghabon ng backlog at malaki po ang magagawa kung ma-restore yung pondo.

**[01:57:07.630] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001831)  
Hindi nga lang ma-restore dun sa previously approved, pero matiyak po natin na madadagdagan yung pondo

**[01:57:13.810] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001832)  
para sa human rights protection promotion, education, at mga policy-related programs.

**[01:57:20.670] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001833)  
Bilang panghuli po sa kalagayan ng mga human rights defenders, may mga kababaihan po na dumulog at naghahin ng complaint,

**[01:57:28.370] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001834)  
ng reklamo sa harap ng CHR.

**[01:57:30.510] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001835)  
Tungkol po sa presence ng mga civil-military operations battalion dito mismo sa National Capital Region

**[01:57:40.510] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001836)  
na nagkakaroon po ng mga kaso ng profiling, surveillance, at threats at intimidation.

**[01:57:50.970] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001837)  
Meron na po bang ulat ang CHR tungkol dito?

**[01:57:53.930] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001838)  
At ano po po yung mga kailangan natin para mapalakas po yung paggampa ng mandato?

**[01:58:00.510] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001839)  
Ang CHR pagdating sa ganitong tipo ng mga kaso?

**[01:58:03.970] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001840)  
Wala po akong actual data sa ngayon nung tinutukoy po ninyong kaso or insidente.

**[01:58:10.330] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001841)  
We'll just provide it to you, Your Honor, Madam Chair.

**[01:58:14.370] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001842)  
Kung yung pong tinatanong po ninyo, ano pa ang dapat gawin upang mapalakas?

**[01:58:21.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001843)  
Ang isa po sa mga pinaplano ng komisyon ay isang national preventive mechanism,

**[01:58:26.410] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001844)  
national protection mechanism.

**[01:58:29.390] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001845)  
Tatlo pong pinaplano.

**[01:58:30.510] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001846)  
Pillars ito ng protection mechanism for human rights defenders.

**[01:58:36.710] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001847)  
Pillar one would be the early warning system o tawag po namin ay bantay

**[01:58:40.950] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001848)  
upang agad-agad naming matukoy ang mga human rights violations at agad din kami makatugon.

**[01:58:51.430] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001849)  
Ang pangalawa pong pillar nito ay the protection mechanism itself.

**[01:58:55.870] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001850)  
Yun po yung mga existing activities and programs of the commission.

**[01:59:01.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001851)  
Which include investigation, witness protection and support to victims.

**[01:59:09.890] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001852)  
At ang pangatlo po yung national monitoring mechanism

**[01:59:14.030] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001853)  
kung saan ang tawag po namin sa pillar na yun ay tugon or gabay.

**[01:59:24.510] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001854)  
Ang objective po nun ay policy recommendation.

**[01:59:28.510] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001855)  
Ang objective po ay pulungin ang mga stakeholders including government agencies and civil society

**[01:59:43.630] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001856)  
upang pag-usapan kung ano ang problema sa karapatang pantao at hanapan ng solusyon

**[01:59:51.650] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001857)  
kung ano man yung pupwedeng tugunan agad-agad or ano yung pupwedeng unti-unting ma-address.

**[02:00:00.000] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001858)  
ating pamahalaan. So, three prong

**[02:00:02.180] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001859)  
tatlopong pillars ng National

**[02:00:04.100] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001860)  
Protection Mechanism. Hopefully,

**[02:00:07.000] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001861)  
sa ngayon po kasi

**[02:00:08.500] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001862)  
ginagawa na namin ito,

**[02:00:10.220] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001863)  
iniintroduce na namin itong

**[02:00:11.620] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001864)  
protection mechanism na ito, and hopefully

**[02:00:14.320] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001865)  
we could be successful

**[02:00:15.980] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001866)  
in implementing them.

**[02:00:19.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001867)  
Madam Chair, pang huli na po,

**[02:00:21.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001868)  
papayagan po ba ng

**[02:00:22.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001869)  
CHR itong mga ulat,

**[02:00:24.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001870)  
halimbawa, ng presence po ng

**[02:00:26.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001871)  
armed personnel,

**[02:00:27.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001872)  
sa kalagayan po na ito, halimbawa,

**[02:00:30.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001873)  
yung 12 CMO ng AFP

**[02:00:31.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001874)  
sa daycare centers,

**[02:00:33.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001875)  
at sa iba pong mga civilian facilities,

**[02:00:36.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001876)  
kung wala pong

**[02:00:37.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001877)  
aksyon dito ang CHR,

**[02:00:40.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001878)  
ano ang maaaring gawin

**[02:00:42.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001879)  
para matiyak yung

**[02:00:43.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001880)  
agara na aksyon ng CHR

**[02:00:45.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001881)  
sa ganitong mga ulat?

**[02:00:49.420] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001882)  
Ang general position po ng

**[02:00:51.500] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001883)  
Commission on the presence

**[02:00:53.780] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001884)  
of military personnel

**[02:00:55.320] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001885)  
sa mga civilian facilities

**[02:00:57.620] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001886)  
is we discourage

**[02:00:59.800] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001887)  
yung kanilang presence.

**[02:01:01.420] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001888)  
Dahil wala naman pong kinalaman

**[02:01:03.380] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001889)  
ang military operations

**[02:01:05.320] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001890)  
sa isang civilian facility.

**[02:01:09.480] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001891)  
Pero hindi,

**[02:01:11.660] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001892)  
tungkol po, if ever,

**[02:01:13.280] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001893)  
meron pong mga specific incident dito.

**[02:01:15.880] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001894)  
Iniimbestigan po ng Commission yan.

**[02:01:18.720] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001895)  
And kung

**[02:01:19.200] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001896)  
kinakailangan,

**[02:01:21.920] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001897)  
pinaparating din po namin yan

**[02:01:23.540] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001898)  
sa aming

**[02:01:24.900] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001899)  
kapartner sa Armed Forces

**[02:01:27.400] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001900)  
of the Philippines, yung ating,

**[02:01:28.880] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001901)  
yung pong kanilang

**[02:01:30.100] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001902)  
AFP, Center for Law

**[02:01:32.980] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001903)  
on Armed Conflict,

**[02:01:35.380] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001904)  
kung saan meron po kaming kasunduan

**[02:01:37.340] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001905)  
on case referral

**[02:01:38.740] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001906)  
and human rights promotion

**[02:01:41.360] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-001907)  
partnership.

**[02:01:45.110] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001908)  
Madam Chair, hindi po sapat

**[02:01:46.490] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001909)  
ang pag-discourage. Kailangan po talaga

**[02:01:48.530] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001910)  
lagyan pa natin ng

**[02:01:50.190] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001911)  
higit na ngipe, ng higit na pangil,

**[02:01:53.010] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001912)  
ng higit po na lakas

**[02:01:54.550] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001913)  
noong CHR sa pag-titiyak

**[02:01:56.730] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001914)  
na wala hong abuso

**[02:01:57.950] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001915)  
at maiiwasan natin

**[02:02:00.630] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001916)  
ang mga kaso.

**[02:02:01.630] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001917)  
Ang paglabag sa karapatang

**[02:02:02.750] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001918)  
pantao. Kaya po, ang supportan ng representasyon

**[02:02:05.090] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001919)  
na ito, hindi lang po sa

**[02:02:06.130] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001920)  
papataas ng pondo, kundi sa

**[02:02:08.250] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001921)  
pagpapalakas pa ng CHR

**[02:02:10.610] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001922)  
bilang ating natatangin

**[02:02:12.570] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001923)  
na pambansa na human rights

**[02:02:14.510] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001924)  
institution. Maraming salamat

**[02:02:16.290] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001925)  
Madam Chair. Maraming salamat.

**[02:02:18.510] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-001926)  
Maraming salamat po, Representative

**[02:02:20.510] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-001927)

**[02:02:22.710] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-001928)

**[02:02:24.970] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-001929)

**[02:02:27.350] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-001930)  
Representative Antonio

**[02:02:28.510] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-001931)  
Tineo, you are recognized.

**[02:02:31.630] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001933)  
Chair,

**[02:02:32.630] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001934)  
Chair, yung tanong ko, kaugnay sa

inulunsad na investigasyon

**[02:02:55.270] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001944)  
sa

**[02:02:59.810] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001946)

ng Duterte.

Sa panahong ito,

**[02:03:07.150] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001950)  
ay nasa ICC na

**[02:03:09.030] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001951)  
si dating
