**[02:15:34.280] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002463)  
For Fiscal Year 2027, Budget Shortfall and Requested Augmentation.

**[02:15:40.920] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002464)  
Galing po ito sa Caucus of Development NGO Networks.

**[02:15:46.860] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002465)  
May the CHR state for the record the total budget it proposed to the DBM for Fiscal Year 2027

**[02:15:54.440] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002466)  
and the amount recommended for the CHR.

**[02:15:58.440] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002467)  
Under the 2027 National Expenditure Program, what is the resulting shortfall?

**[02:16:05.220] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002468)  
Both in peso and percentage terms, and what reason, if any, did the DBM communicate for the reduction or exclusion?

**[02:16:16.520] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002469)  
Which proposed expenditures under personal services, MOEs, and capital outlays were deducted or excluded?

**[02:16:25.920] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002470)  
What concrete effects will this have on

**[02:16:28.440] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002471)  
CHR's investigations, legal and protection assistance,

**[02:16:34.220] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002472)  
detention facility visits, human rights education, regional operations, and response to urgent cases.

**[02:16:42.760] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002473)  
Which items does the CHR respectfully request Congress to restore or augment?

**[02:16:48.960] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002474)  
If what specific amounts and what measurable improvements in services and outputs

**[02:16:55.720] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002475)  
would additional funding

**[02:16:58.440] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002476)  
enable?

**[02:16:59.480] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002477)  
You are recognized, Chairperson Richard Palpal-latoc.

**[02:17:03.040] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002478)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:17:04.980] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002479)  
Can you?

**[02:17:07.240] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002480)  
So, for the resulting shortfall,

**[02:17:16.130] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002481)  
from tier 1,

**[02:17:22.230] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002482)  
1 billion 173 million 237.

**[02:17:29.530] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002483)  
Sa NEP

**[02:17:33.080] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002484)  
1.272 billion pesos.

**[02:17:36.380] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002485)  
Pero po sa 2026 GAA, kung kukumpara po natin, ay 1.374 billion.

**[02:17:51.620] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002486)  
Sa tier 2 naman, or 0.02%, ay hindi.

**[02:17:57.620] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002487)  
Sorry, I'm sorry Madam Chair.

**[02:17:58.960] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002488)  
So mayroon pong variance na from the total proposal, a reduction of 53% from the NEP.

**[02:18:25.820] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002489)  
Dahil po ang hiningi po ng Commission ay 2.749 billion, ang binigay po sa NEP ay 1.272 billion.

**[02:18:39.480] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002490)  
Ang natanggal po dito, naapektoan po dito ay capital outlay na 152 million affecting infrastructure, ICT, equipment, software, and transport or vehicle, motor vehicle.

**[02:19:03.200] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002491)  
Then sa MOOE, nabawasan po siya ng 34 million.

**[02:19:12.620] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002492)  
Due to decrease in the ISSP, then, so a total of 152 million for capital outlay, then for MOOE, 34 million po.

**[02:19:39.660] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002493)  
May namiss pa po ba?

**[02:19:40.720] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002494)  
Okay, for our next question, pa rin po, from Caucus of Development NGO Networks.

**[02:19:49.970] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002495)  
Staffing and operational capacity.

**[02:19:52.410] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002496)  
May the CHR provide the authorized filled and vacant work positions in each central and regional office,

**[02:20:02.670] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002497)  
including the staffing complement and workload of each regional office,

**[02:20:07.850] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002498)  
and which regions are operating below the minimum staffing required to discharge CHR mandate effectively.

**[02:20:15.170] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002499)  
How does insufficient staffing affect response times, case backlogs,

**[02:20:21.770] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002500)  
investigation,

**[02:20:22.410] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002501)  
monitoring coverage,

**[02:20:25.250] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002502)  
and services for victims of human rights violations?

**[02:20:29.170] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002503)  
What additional positions and corresponding FY2027 appropriation are required to provide every regional office with an adequate operational complement?

**[02:20:42.790] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002504)  
Mr. Chairperson Richard Palpal-latoc, you are recognized.

**[02:20:46.570] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002505)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:20:47.310] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002506)  
So, the CHR's constitutional mandate,

**[02:20:52.410] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002507)  
is nationwide.

**[02:20:54.630] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002508)  
But,

**[02:20:54.990] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002509)  
its effectiveness depends heavily on the capacity of its regional offices,

**[02:20:59.810] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002510)  
which are often the first point of contact for victims,

**[02:21:03.890] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002511)  
families,

**[02:21:04.530] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002512)  
human rights defenders,

**[02:21:05.790] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002513)  
persons deprived of liberty,

**[02:21:08.390] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002514)  
local governments,

**[02:21:09.410] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002515)  
and communities.

**[02:21:11.910] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002516)  
So,

**[02:21:12.830] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002517)  
definitely,

**[02:21:13.530] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002518)  
insufficient staffing can directly affect the speed of complaint intake,

**[02:21:17.650] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002519)  
investigation,

**[02:21:18.450] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002520)  
legal assessment,

**[02:21:19.350] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002521)  
field monitoring,

**[02:21:21.170] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002522)  
detention visits,

**[02:21:22.290] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002523)  
human rights,

**[02:21:22.530] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002524)  
education,

**[02:21:23.550] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002525)  
report preparation,

**[02:21:24.650] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002526)  
and follow-up with duty bearers.

**[02:21:29.260] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002527)  
So,

**[02:21:32.330] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002528)  
in 2026,

**[02:21:35.670] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002529)  
the Commission was able to significantly reduce the backlog cases due to the firm guidance of the Commission.

**[02:21:44.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002530)  
However,

**[02:21:45.090] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002531)  
the regional offices still fall behind with considerable amount of backlog cases.

**[02:21:49.590] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002532)  
Due to lack of manpower,

**[02:21:53.590] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002533)  
which is one of the reasons,

**[02:21:57.970] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002534)  
so,

**[02:21:58.430] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002535)  
ito pong mga region na ito,

**[02:21:59.610] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002536)  
Region 4B,

**[02:22:00.990] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002537)  
Region 7,

**[02:22:02.270] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002538)  
and

**[02:22:02.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002539)  
Negros Island Region.

**[02:22:06.530] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002540)  
In the case of Region 4B,

**[02:22:09.750] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002541)  
the geographical challenges has been one of the major hindrances,

**[02:22:13.950] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002542)  
kasi po,

**[02:22:14.370] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002543)  
island provinces po,

**[02:22:15.610] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002544)  
karamihan ng mga,

**[02:22:17.390] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002545)  
ng jurisdiction ito.

**[02:22:21.470] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002546)  
And then,

**[02:22:21.990] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002547)  
investigators and lawyers have to travel

**[02:22:23.570] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002548)  
from the central office to investigate.

**[02:22:28.320] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002549)  
Then,

**[02:22:29.100] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002550)  
for insufficient staffing directly,

**[02:22:38.780] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002551)  
limits the capacity of regional offices to respond promptly and consistently to alleged human rights violations.

**[02:22:46.900] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002552)  
So,

**[02:22:47.080] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002553)  
fewer investigators,

**[02:22:48.420] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002554)  
lawyers,

**[02:22:49.800] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002555)  
monitoring officers,

**[02:22:50.940] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002556)  
and victim support personals than required,

**[02:22:53.920] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002557)  
increases initial response and case processing times,

**[02:22:57.480] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002558)  
particularly when personnel must travel to geographic locations,

**[02:23:00.720] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002559)  
particularly dispersed or high-risk areas.

**[02:23:05.970] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002560)  
So,

**[02:23:06.330] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002561)  
the staffing situation of the regional office may not be fully reflected

**[02:23:10.310] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002562)  
by the number of vacant positions alone.

**[02:23:14.090] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002563)  
Even where substantially all existing authorized positions are filled,

**[02:23:19.710] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002564)  
there remains an opportunity to improve operational capacity

**[02:23:22.730] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002565)  
through a more balanced distribution of technical,

**[02:23:26.610] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002566)  
professional,

**[02:23:27.230] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002567)  
and administrative support personnel.

**[02:23:30.590] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002568)  
So,

**[02:23:30.910] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002569)  
in this respect,

**[02:23:31.870] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002570)  
the concern is less,

**[02:23:32.810] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002571)  
the existence of vacancies and more,

**[02:23:35.390] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002572)  
the extent to which the present staffing pattern allows substantive personnel

**[02:23:39.250] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002573)  
to concentrate on the functions for which they are primarily appointed.

**[02:23:44.790] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002574)  
So,

**[02:23:45.150] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002575)  
we'll just,

**[02:23:45.970] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002576)  
if the chairperson would allow,

**[02:23:48.390] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002577)  
we'll just submit the numbers of the personnel actually occupying the position in different regions.

**[02:23:56.290] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002578)  
Okay,

**[02:23:56.650] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002579)  
noted on that,

**[02:23:57.650] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002580)  
chairperson.

**[02:23:59.730] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002581)  
For our last question,

**[02:24:01.630] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002582)  
po.

**[02:24:02.810] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002583)  
From Caucus of Development NGO Networks,

**[02:24:05.790] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002584)  
ma rin po.

**[02:24:07.770] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002585)  
CHR oversight on NTF,

**[02:24:10.670] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002586)  
ELCAC,

**[02:24:11.310] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002587)  
and the support to the Barangay Development Program.

**[02:24:15.150] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002588)  
The fiscal year 2027 NEP proposes approximately

**[02:24:19.390] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002589)  
9.74 billion for the local government support fund,

**[02:24:25.130] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002590)  
support to the Barangay Development Program of the NTF-ELCAC.

**[02:24:30.330] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002591)  
Considering the scale and geographical,

**[02:24:32.810] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002592)  
reach of this program,

**[02:24:34.470] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002593)  
what additional demands does its implementation place on CHR monitoring,

**[02:24:41.050] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002594)  
investigation,

**[02:24:43.050] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002595)  
protection and human rights education functions,

**[02:24:46.050] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002596)  
particularly on conflict-affected and remote communities?

**[02:24:50.570] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002597)  
Based on CHR monitoring and cases,

**[02:24:53.810] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002598)  
what requiring human rights risks or concern,

**[02:24:56.810] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002599)  
if any,

**[02:24:57.810] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002600)  
should Congress consider in reviewing this appropriation?

**[02:25:02.810] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002601)  
Does CHR have sufficient personnel and operational resources,

**[02:25:08.190] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002602)  
especially in its regional offices,

**[02:25:10.470] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002603)  
to undertake independent monitoring,

**[02:25:14.070] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002604)  
investigate complaints,

**[02:25:16.010] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002605)  
assist affected persons and engage communities?

**[02:25:20.550] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002606)  
If not,

**[02:25:21.470] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002607)  
what specific additional appropriation does the CHR require?

**[02:25:26.470] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002608)  
Finally,

**[02:25:27.730] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002609)  
what safeguards,

**[02:25:28.750] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002610)  
transparency,

**[02:25:30.310] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002611)  
measures and reporting requirements,

**[02:25:32.590] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002612)  
does the CHR recommend that Congress include

**[02:25:36.350] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002613)  
in the relevant appropriations to ensure objective beneficiary selection,

**[02:25:41.670] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002614)  
non-discrimination,

**[02:25:43.470] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002615)  
due process,

**[02:25:44.970] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002616)  
meaningful community participation,

**[02:25:47.790] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002617)  
protection against red-tagging,

**[02:25:49.910] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002618)  
and retaliation,

**[02:25:51.770] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002619)  
effective access,

**[02:25:52.990] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002620)  
and oversight by the CHR?

**[02:25:56.450] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002621)  
You are recognized.

**[02:25:57.930] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002622)  
Chairperson Richard Palpal-latoc.

**[02:25:59.690] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002623)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:26:00.930] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002624)  
So, as men, as...

**[02:26:02.590] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002625)  
Already answered before when Congressman Laila de Lima asked.

**[02:26:10.550] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002626)  
We don't have, the CHR does not have oversight function over the NTF-ELCAC.

**[02:26:16.130] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002627)  
So we don't have administrative or programmatic oversight of the NTF-ELCAC or the support to the Barangay Development Program.

**[02:26:26.550] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002628)  
The NTF-ELCAC is an executive mechanism and the implementation and financial administration of the support to the Barangay Development Program are functions of the executive branch.

**[02:26:41.750] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002629)  
What we can do is, as I said, monitor the compliance of the government to human rights obligations

**[02:26:51.650] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002630)  
and ensure that in the performance of the NTF-ELCAC,

**[02:26:57.830] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002631)  
of its mandate, there's no human rights violation committed.

**[02:27:04.150] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002632)  
And then what additional demands?

**[02:27:06.630] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002633)  
The principal additional demand of the CHR is not the monitoring of the SBDP as a program,

**[02:27:17.490] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002634)  
but the increased possibility of human rights concern arising in geographically isolated,

**[02:27:22.810] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002635)  
conflict-affected, and otherwise vulnerable communities.

**[02:27:27.110] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002636)  
Where access to

**[02:27:27.830] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002637)  
Government institutions and remedies may already be difficult.

**[02:27:32.690] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002638)  
So in practical terms, this may require CHR's regional offices to undertake more field-based monitoring,

**[02:27:40.870] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002639)  
receive and assess complaints, conduct fact-finding and field investigations,

**[02:27:46.330] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002640)  
provide or facilitate appropriate legal and protective assistance,

**[02:27:50.030] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002641)  
document alleged violation, and conduct human rights education and community engagement.

**[02:27:55.510] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002642)  
Based on the CHR's monitoring,

**[02:27:59.370] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002643)  
complaints and public inquiries,

**[02:28:01.530] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002644)  
the recurring risks that Congress should consider may include

**[02:28:04.350] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002645)  
widespread and systemic vilification of human rights defenders

**[02:28:08.750] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002646)  
or red-tagging involving malicious utterances or unfounded information

**[02:28:13.650] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002647)  
that imputes one's participation in a criminal act or involvement in an ongoing armed conflict,

**[02:28:20.770] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002648)  
ultimately impacting the right to life, liberty, security, privacy,

**[02:28:24.950] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002649)  
and the freedom of expression and association.

**[02:28:29.110] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002650)  
And the staffing and budget,

**[02:28:34.690] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002651)  
we reiterate our manifestation a while ago regarding our Tier 2 proposal, Your Honor, Madam Chair.

**[02:28:42.230] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002652)  
Thank you.

**[02:28:42.890] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002653)  
Thank you, Chairperson Richard Palpal-latoc.

**[02:28:50.430] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002654)  
Yan na po yung last question natin for this afternoon.

**[02:28:54.230] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002655)  
Wala na po bang hahabol? I think so.

**[02:28:57.010] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002656)  
But before we terminate our hearing for today,

**[02:29:00.030] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002657)  
our Chairperson, Honorable Mika Suansing, sends her apologies for not being able to join us today.

**[02:29:08.790] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002658)  
She is currently attending a meeting in Malacanang.

**[02:29:12.330] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002659)  
She has requested the Commission of Human Rights to submit,

**[02:29:15.890] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002660)  
ito na po yung hinihintay natin,

**[02:29:18.150] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002661)  
its wish list or request in consolidation with the Human Rights Violation Victims Memorial Commission

**[02:29:26.070] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002662)  
by Wednesday, September 9, with the items

**[02:29:30.030] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002663)  
prioritized accordingly.

**[02:29:32.030] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002664)  
So, sa ating pong CHR family,

**[02:29:36.030] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002665)  
looking forward po kami na for next week.

**[02:29:39.030] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002666)  
ma-submit ninyo po sa amin.

**[02:29:41.030] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002667)  
At yan po ay pagtutulong-tulungan natin na sana hindi lang maipasa,

**[02:29:45.030] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002668)  
kundi madagdagan.

**[02:29:47.030] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002669)  
Makasama sa Tier 2 sa NEP ang budget ng CHR.

**[02:29:54.570] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002670)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:29:56.570] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002671)  
We will gladly comply.

**[02:29:58.570] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002672)  
Yes.

**[02:30:01.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002673)  
Madam Chair?

**[02:30:02.280] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002673-split-2)  
Yes, Representative Tinio.

**[02:30:08.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002673-split-3)  
Yes, Madam Chair, I move that the Committee terminate its hearing on the proposed Fiscal Year 2027 Budget of the Commission on Human Rights,

**[02:30:18.830] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002674)  
including the Human Rights Violations Victims Memorial Commission under House Bill No. 10858.

**[02:30:26.390] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002675)  
I so move, Madam Chair.

**[02:30:29.190] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002676)  
I second the motion, Madam Chair.

**[02:30:30.390] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002677)  
Thank you, Representative Elago.

**[02:30:31.990] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002678)  
It has been moved and duly seconded to terminate the hearing of the proposed Fiscal Year 2027 Budget of the Commission on Human Rights,

**[02:30:43.430] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002679)  
including the Human Rights Violations Victims Memorial Commission.

**[02:30:49.950] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002680)  
Is there any objection?

**[02:30:53.350] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002681)  
None. The Chair hears none.

**[02:30:55.870] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002682)  
The motion is approved.

**[02:30:59.610] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002683)  
Thank you. Thank you, Your Honors and our colleagues.

**[02:31:03.590] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002684)  
From the Commission on Human Rights and also po sa lahat po ng participation for today's budget hearing,

**[02:31:09.430] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002685)  
kay Representative Tinio and Representative Elago,

**[02:31:14.070] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002686)  
for the suspension of the Committee hearing until next scheduled budget hearing,

**[02:31:20.430] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002687)  
Madam Chair, I move that we suspend the Committee hearing at the Andaya Hall on House Bill 10858

**[02:31:26.630] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002688)  
until Monday, September 7, 2026 at 9 a.m.

**[02:31:30.670] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002689)  
I so move, Madam Chair.

**[02:31:32.750] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002690)  
I second the motion.

**[02:31:33.590] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002691)  
Madam Chair.

**[02:31:34.590] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002692)  
It has been moved and duly seconded.

**[02:31:37.470] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002693)  
Is there any objection?

**[02:31:40.710] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002694)  
The Chair hears none.

**[02:31:42.310] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002695)  
The Committee hearing on House Bill No. 10858 here at the Andaya Hall is hereby suspended until Monday, September 7, 2026 at 9 a.m.

**[02:31:57.130] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002696)  
Thank you.

**[02:31:58.310] CHR Chairperson Richard P. Palpal-latoc** (asr-002697)  
Maraming salamat po Cong. Madam Chair.

**[02:32:00.950] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-002698)  
Thank you po
